Use the async forms: `contentToYUpdateAsync`, `yDocToHtmlAsync`, `prepareExternalContent` → `applyExternalContent(doc, kind, content, prepared)`, `resolveSuggestionsInHtmlAsync`, `suggestionViewOfHtml`, and `ensureRenderedSize(doc)` + `renderedSizeOf(doc)` for "how big is this live page" (the command route and block moves measure off-thread before their synchronous section).
- **A note that cannot be converted has NO live document.** `loadDocumentState` throws `DocumentTooComplexError` (reason `too_complex: …`) — nothing derived from the body enters a Y.Doc, `collab_docs` or any client's local store (an earlier design seeded a read-only plain-text Y.Doc under a reserved client id; browsers persisted it to IndexedDB and were then locked out after the note healed — removed). The socket is answered `too_complex`; `CollabDoc` shows `PlainTextPage` (the stored note over REST as plain text — never parsed in the browser either — with "Edit as plain text" for edit/own: a compare-and-set PATCH; fixing the content brings the live editor back in the same browser, nothing to purge). `busy:` = the load found no converter slot → "try again". Direct connections get the error: command endpoint 413 `document_too_large`, MCP comment/suggest tools `invalid_request`, block moves 413 `too_complex`. A failed load consumes no unconfirmed command receipts and destroys the Hocuspocus `Document` itself (Hocuspocus never does for a load that threw — its Awareness interval leaked).
- **A LIVE document whose note becomes unconvertible is BLOCKED** (`isDocBlocked`): never written to the vault again (the newer note wins), sockets dropped and new ones refused `too_complex`, so it unloads (the flag goes with it); `isDocLive`/`liveDocument` treat it as not live. Blocking needs a pre-check refusal or the SAME content failing twice (`timeout`/`failed`) — one load-dependent failure is retried.
- **What a snapshot is built on — ONE persisted rule (`collab_docs`, `db.ts`; `vaultRelation` in `collab.ts`), applied identically at load, in the reconciler and in the store.** Row = `state` (latest durable Yjs state), `source_updated_at` + `base_hash` (the vault version and CONTENT hash it is built on), `ahead` (0: `state` IS the vault content; 1: it holds changes the vault lacks), `base_state` (while ahead: the Yjs state that IS the vault content — the true base of every three-way merge; NULL = unknown), `attempts` (hashes of writes SENT but not confirmed) + `attempt_state`. A newer vault copy is `metadata` (same hash → only the stamp moves; never folded), `ours` (hash of an attempted write → a lost acknowledgement; adopted, never folded), or `external` (merged). Rows from before the columns (`base_hash` NULL) fall back to the timestamp rule. Never decide "externally edited" by `updatedAt` alone, and never save a state as in-step (`saveDocState`) unless it IS the vault content. **External edit + unconfirmed attempts (round 4, H1):** the row's `base_state` may be STALE — our write landed (acknowledgement lost or still in flight) and someone edited on top of it; merging against the old base re-created what was typed in between (`start / edit one / EXTERNAL / edit one`). `landedAttempt` asks the note's history (`/versions`, newest 6, back to the row's own source) for a version whose content hashes to an attempt; `mergeBases` → that attempt's state, the row's base (history complete, no attempt in it), or — history unavailable / an older attempt — BOTH candidates: `foldVaultContent` then tries first the NEWEST candidate the vault's content merely adds to (our write is in there verbatim: it landed), else the OLDER one (a too-new base would silently delete unsent typing; a too-old one only duplicates, which is caught), runs the merge on a copy, and refuses a result holding more copies of a block than either side had (`hasDuplicatedBlocks`) — no candidate passes → the vault's content replaces the document + the `external-replaced` notice. `confirmDocAttempt` is a no-op on a row that no longer records the attempt (it was rebased meanwhile). **Round 5 (M-3/M-4/M-6) replaces the guessing:** `landedAttempt` = ONE listing of the newest 50 versions + at most 8 bodies, each vault call ≤ `collabTuning.historyCallMs` (2.5 s) and the whole lookup ≤ `historyDeadlineMs` (6 s, then "cannot tell"); attempts record their size (`collab_docs.attempt_lens`), so versions of another size are not fetched once one fetched body confirmed what the listing's `content_len` means; the reconciler finishes lookups after every other document had its turn. `mergeBases` → `{candidates, uncertain, floor}` (`uncertain` even with one candidate). An uncertain fold tries each candidate on a copy and takes it only if `mergeIsSane` (documents AND code, per word + per attribute-bearing leaf: never more than the fuller side has; nothing both sides hold is gone; nothing only the document has beyond the floor — unsent typing — is gone; nothing only the vault has beyond the floor is gone) — else **nobody guesses**: the vault's copy replaces the document's, every socket gets `external-replaced`, and what the document held is SET ASIDE (`collab_set_aside`: plain-text body + exact Yjs state, newest 5 per note, 90 days; also for the no-base replacement of an ahead snapshot, sheets and canvases included). **Round 6 (B3) — which candidate is tried:** the floor (the row's base) is always safe to try (too old only duplicates); the state of an UNCONFIRMED write is a base only if that write can be seen in the vault's copy (`attemptInVault`: every word it added over the floor is there at least as often, AND the run of text between the floor's and the write's common prefix/suffix occurs in the vault's plain text more often than in the floor's) → `present`: only the write's state is tried (the floor would double it); `absent`: only the floor (a too-new base deletes typing the vault never saw — the old check passed that whenever each typed WORD stood somewhere in the vault's copy); `nothing_added`: floor, then the write's state. `mergeIsSane(base, floor, local, vault, merged)` per word: `merged ≤ base + (local − base)⁺ + (vault − base)⁺` (was `max(local, vault)`: refused two sides adding the same common word) and `merged ≥ min(l, v) + (l − max(f, v))⁺ + (v − max(f, l))⁺` when both hold it (the floor used to be ignored there), else `(max(l, v) − f)⁺`. `attemptInVault` counts the run with a linear matcher (`occurrences`, KMP — `indexOf` was n × m on periodic text). **S3:** the set-aside row is written BEFORE the document is replaced (`keep` callback of `foldVaultContent`); if the insert fails nothing is replaced (`SetAsideFailedError`; retried per document with a backoff `collabTuning.setAsideRetryMs` 5 s doubling to 5 min, and the page's sockets get one `prism:unsaved pending` per episode): the reconciler retries next tick, the store records a retry (and does not write the page over the note), a load opens the snapshot as it was and leaves the note's content as news. **S2:** a store whose write got a DEFINITE refusal (409, any 4xx but 408) drops that attempt (`dropDocAttempt`) — only when it is the SOLE recorded attempt (with an older unanswered one the row's kept state is the refused attempt's: dropping the hash made that state the certain base once the older write turned out to have landed, and later folds deleted the difference silently — S-1) and unless the same content was already sent once without an answer (that send may have landed); only outcome-unknown failures (no answer, 5xx, 408) keep an attempt, so ordinary concurrent editing merges on a CERTAIN base.
- **Stores (`storeDocumentState`) — invariants.** (1) Snapshot state + content hash are saved (`saveDocAttempt`) BEFORE the vault write is sent; a confirmed write (`confirmDocAttempt`) stores exactly the state that was rendered (`ahead = 0`). (2) The write is compare-and-set on the version this store read; a 409 → re-read → `vaultRelation` → re-render, ≤ 3 times. The store's render is awaited: if the row's `source_updated_at` moved meanwhile (the reconciler merged an external edit — state, source and base move together) the snapshot is OLDER than the row and is not saved (`saveDocAttempt(…, expectSource)` returns false; the store re-reads and snapshots again — M3). A note whose version is known but which cannot be READ now is not written (no unconditioned overwrite). (3) An external body change is merged THREE-WAY against the true base (`foldVaultContent`: the change is applied to a fork of the base, only its delta reaches the document — both sides survive; documents + code). No known base (legacy row, sheets, canvas) → the vault content replaces the document and clients get `prism:notice external-replaced`. (4) A store that wrote nothing saves the latest state AHEAD (`saveDocAhead`: base, source and attempts kept — never a null source) and records the note in `collab_unsaved {reason, permanent, attempts, last_attempt, next_attempt}`. Load-dependent causes (converter busy/timeout/crash, vault 5xx/unreachable, repeated 409, unreadable) are retried: a timer while loaded (`storeRetryDelayMs`: 3 s, 10 s, 30 s, 60 s, then doubling — capped at 30 min for `timeout`/`failed`, 5 min otherwise; also armed when a snapshot is loaded while its note is UNREADABLE, and the sweep stores a loaded document that has a row but no timer — L2), `sweepUnsavedDocuments` after an unload (60 s; least-recently-attempted first, per-row backoff 1 min → 6 h, a deleted note drops its row, after 14 days → `gave_up`, kept + logged), and at the next load. Causes retrying cannot fix (render beyond `CONVERT_MAX_NODES`, a rendered body the load pre-check would refuse, vault 400/413/422) are `permanent`: not retried, and everyone on the page is told by a Hocuspocus stateless message `{type:"prism:unsaved", state:"unsaved"|"pending"|"saved", reason}` (sent on change and to every socket on connect; `pending` = a retried row — older clients ignore it) → `CollabDoc` shows "Not saved to the page" + `unsavedExplanation(reason)` (copy by reason: "smaller" only for converter refusals and vault 413) and reports sync source `unsaved` (badge never "Saved"; not a button — `syncBadgeAction`) or, while `pending`, `retrying` ("Saving to the page — retrying"); the one-off `prism:notice` raised at load reaches only sockets connecting within `collabTuning.noticeTtlMs` (15 s); the Yjs state stays in SQLite and opens with the page; the next store that lands clears it. `/acl/workers` gains a `collab` source while rows exist (stale after 1 h, failing when any is permanent). (5) **Body writers outside the live document must ask `hasLiveState(vault, note)` (loaded OR `collab_unsaved`), not `isDocLive`**: `settleUnsaved` gives the snapshot one chance to be written (load + store) and returns `clear | pending | unloadable`; `pending` → go through the live document or answer `409 conflict {live, retry}` — gateway content PATCH/PUT + restore (middleware in `routes/api.ts`, owner passthrough included), MCP `prism_update_note`/restore, block moves (200 only once the change is durable), the import, the review queue (now compare-and-set). **`permanent`** (the row is permanent: vault 400/413/422, `gave_up`, too many nodes) → `409 {error:"unsaved_permanent", retry:false, reason, detail}` (`unsavedPermanentBody`) on all of those; `prism_update_note`/restore answer `conflict` with `detail.retry === false` and "Do NOT retry" and never merge into such a document. **Way out:** `GET /api/admin/collab/unsaved` (rows of the caller's vault — filtered before the limit — plus `setAside[]`; `GET|DELETE /api/admin/collab/set-aside/:id` = one set-aside body — 🔒 **a deliberate exception to "the owner's own view"**: the body is page text as its live document held it, possibly ANOTHER member's private page; kept because it is the only recovery path, so: server owner by email, human origin only (session/device; MCP + loopback token → 403 `agent_origin_refused`), and every read/delete — refused ones too — writes an `action_audit` row `admin.collab-set-aside-read|delete` with ids/sizes only. Rows go after 90 days (pruned on insert AND by the 60 s unsaved sweep) and with their note: `deleteCollabSetAsideForNote` on `DELETE /trash/:id`, the trash purge worker, the member DELETE, the owner passthrough DELETE (a PATH alias is resolved to the id first, only when the vault holds any row) and the sweep's "note deleted") + `POST /api/admin/collab/unsaved/:id/discard {confirm:true, force?:true}` (server owner by email, CSRF, human origin; `action_audit` `admin.collab-discard-unsaved`, counts only) → `discardUnsavedChanges(vault, note, {force})`: the note's body replaces the document's IN PLACE (snapshot kept, same Yjs history — a browser holding the old state converges on the stored page instead of merging a second copy), on the loaded document when open. **Round 5 (M-5):** only a PERMANENT row without `force` (a retried one → 409 `not_permanent`); open sockets get `prism:notice unsaved-discarded`; it runs inside the document's Hocuspocus `saveMutex` and decides after an in-flight store finished (that store used to write the discarded text afterwards); a document that is loading → `busy`. A browser that typed OFFLINE after that still brings its own typing back. **The gateway middleware settles (load + store) only for a caller with `edit` on an unlocked, non-system note** (a viewer gets the route's own 403 with nothing loaded — M1), at most `UNSAVED_SETTLES_PER_MINUTE` (6) per actor; past it → the 409 + `Retry-After` with no further attempt. `prism_update_note` obeys the same two rules itself (`collabAccess` ≥ edit on THIS note, the same bucket — `unsaved-settle.ts`; round 5, M-1), and `settleUnsaved` answers `permanent` at once for a permanent row whose document is not loaded (M-2). **Rollback (L4/L5):** code from before these columns neither maintains nor clears `base_state` / `attempts` / `attempt_state` / `base_hash` — after running an older build, null them before redeploying this one (`UPDATE collab_docs SET base_state = NULL, attempts = NULL, attempt_state = NULL, attempt_lens = NULL, base_hash = NULL;`): the rows then fall back to the timestamp rule (an external edit replaces, with the notice) instead of merging against a base the old build left stale. Tests: `test/conversion-round3.test.ts` (real Hocuspocus), fixture e2e `collab-too-complex.spec.ts` (the not-saved notice; `RealServer.limits`).
- **Residual risks of this subsystem (round 5, honest list).** (1) The pre-check is a model of three parsers, not the parsers: a shape nobody listed converts inline up to 24 KB / 250 counted nodes / 600 specials — bounded, not proven; the lag tests cover the audited shapes only, on this host's load. (2) Table cells are counted for Markdown; an HTML table's cost is its tags (the server runs no `fixTables`, but a browser opening `colspan="1000000"` is the client's problem). (3) An actor key is whatever the caller passes; conversions without one (discard, sweep loads) are not penalised for timeouts — bounded by the per-content memory and `unconvertible` (same content twice). A member with many accounts/links gets a penalty per key. (4) `mergeIsSane` (uncertain bases only) is still strict below: two sides deleting DIFFERENT copies of one word, a CRDT merge that glues two words, or CJK text without spaces fails it → the vault's copy wins and the page's text is set aside — safe, but a person's unsaved typing leaves the page (it is in `collab_set_aside`, owner-only, API only — **no UI yet**, and nobody is emailed). Since round 6 both sides adding the same word passes (bound relative to the candidate base), so with the floor as base a write that DID land and was then partly rewritten elsewhere shows as near-duplicate text (visible, never a loss; whole doubled blocks are still refused). `attemptInVault` reads "the same short text added on both sides" as our write having landed: one copy survives. Ordinary concurrent editing no longer reaches this path at all (S2). (5) The size shortcut of the history lookup trusts `content_len` after ONE body agreed with it; a vault whose `content_len` is neither characters nor bytes simply gets no shortcut (≤ 8 bodies, then "cannot tell"). (6) A CERTAIN base (history shows the attempt, or no attempts) is merged without the sanity check — as before. (7) A discard that finds the page loading, or loaded meanwhile, answers `busy` (503, retry) rather than waiting. (8) Still on the main thread: `tokenCounts`/`plainBody`/`yDocToDocJson` on the uncertain path (linear; ~0.1–0.2 s for a 2 MB page, rare). (9) `prism_update_note` on a page whose store keeps failing still answers `collab {live, changed}` once merged into the live document (the vault is written later). (10) Round 6: load-path conversions charge nobody; the soft load limit stops ONE actor after 6 failed opens in 10 min (a member with several accounts/links gets 6 each, and 4 worker deaths in a row still open the shared breaker for 30 s). A killer body is refused by hash only after its SECOND death; the memory is per process (a restart forgets). A row with an older unanswered attempt keeps a refused one too → its next external edit is an uncertain merge (round-5 behaviour); when the OLDER attempt lands the base is unknown → the next external edit replaces the page (set aside + notice), never a silent merge. The inline gate's look-ahead bounds (`scanCost`, `blankRun`) came from a sweep of 41 openers × 32 tails and 900 seeded bodies — bounded, not proven: a parser rule nobody tried may still be slow inside 24 KB / 250 nodes / 600 specials. (11) `parseNodes` (the refusal) still knows what ends a table (an empty / spaces-only line): wrong there = a worker timeout, not a stall. (12) A deleted note's `collab_docs` snapshot is NOT removed with it (only its set-aside rows and its `collab_unsaved` row are). (13) The real mid-task worker death (`duringTask`) is exercised only through a fake worker in tests.
- **MCP resource `prism://note/{id}`:** the body is capped (100,000 chars) BEFORE conversion, converted in the worker; fallback = the raw HTML after a one-line `<!-- prism: … could not be converted … -->` note, `mimeType: text/html`, metadata `contentFormat: "html"` + `contentUnconverted`.
- **Still on the main thread, by necessity (linear, measured):** applying a seed / fold to the Y.Doc (`Y.applyUpdate`, `schema.nodeFromJSON` + `updateYFragment`: ~0.2–0.7 s/MB) and `yDocToProsemirrorJSON` (~0.1 s/MB). Tests: `test/conversion.test.ts` (service, pre-check, lanes, fairness, memory, fallbacks, time-bounded pathological inputs with an event-loop probe), `test/conversion-live.test.ts` (real sockets, command endpoint, MCP), `test/conversion-review.test.ts` (stores without a vault write, CAS, metadata-only writes), `test/conversion-round3.test.ts` (unsaved snapshots across loads, true merge base, lost acknowledgements, three-way folds, sweep rotation, REST writers), `test/conversion-round4.test.ts` (C1 shapes with a max-event-loop-lag probe, breaker + backoff, H1 lost-ack + external edit incl. in-paragraph and no-history, M1–M3, L1/L2/L6/L7), `test/conversion-round5.test.ts` (C-1 table + audited shapes at their largest inline size, H-1/H-2 breakers with a 4 MB-heap "dead worker", M-1…M-6, set-aside), `test/conversion-round6.test.ts` (B1/B2/S5 line breaks, tab-blank tables, nesting; every shape × 9 line-break variants measured; the seeded property test; S1 killer memory + actor strike through `setConversionWorkerFactory` fakes; S6 `charge`), `test/collab-round6.test.ts` (B3 too-new base in reconciler/load/store, S2 dropped attempts incl. the same-content guard, S3 set-aside-first with a failing insert, S4 prune/purge/audit, S6 opener not penalised), **the 2 MB / 2 GB-heap test in `conversion.test.ts` runs only with `PRISM_HEAVY_TESTS=1`** (this host is production), `npm run verify:sync -w @prism/web` (not-saved copy + badge action), `test/blocks-append.test.ts` (H4), fixture e2e `collab-too-complex.spec.ts` (degrade → heal → same browser profile). In `test/human-collab.test.ts` a failed vault write is `fv.failNextWrite` (a 500) — a 409 is not a failure for a store any more.

**Collab gotchas (learned the hard way).**
- **Restart pm2 `prism-server` after ANY server change** — it compiles via tsx on start but does NOT hot-reload. A stale server with an older `noteKind` will persist a note through the WRONG path and **corrupt it** (e.g. canvas scene JSON wrapped in `<p>`).
- **The reconciler reads a note only on a change signal (NP-PF-09, `reconcileNeedsRead` in `collab.ts` + `treeRevision` in `tree.ts`).** It used to `getNote` (body included) every loaded document every 2 s — ~92 vault calls/min for three idle tabs. Now a tick reads only when (a) the tree projection's `updatedAt` for the note is NEWER than what the document absorbed (`max(source_updated_at, lastReconciled)` — the one case where the read is not "same"; an OLDER row is our own write's frame in flight: no read), (b) the projection lists no such note (deleted → read every tick, as before), (c) the document has pending state (unconfirmed `attempts`, snapshot `ahead`, a `collab_unsaved` row, a store retry, a failed set-aside), or (d) `COLLAB_RECONCILE_REREAD_MS` (60 s; `collabTuning.reconcileRereadMs`; **`0` = gate off, read every tick**) passed since its last read that ANSWERED and left nothing unfolded (the load counts as one; a failed read, a busy converter or a failed set-aside does not restart the clock — `unsettled`) — a lost socket frame hides an external edit for at most that long. The tick resolves the vault registry once (`treeRevisions()` reader). **Fallback rule: the projection counts only while LIVE** (`loaded && wsLive`, socket open, registry entry unchanged); never built, socket down/reconnecting, snapshot incomplete, `TREE_SUBSCRIBE=0` (every test but `reconciler-gate.test.ts`) → every tick reads, the old behaviour. The gate never builds a projection (a vault nobody loaded the tree for keeps reading). A skip moves no baseline: `markReconciled`, `vaultRelation` and the fold are untouched. A fold leaves the snapshot `ahead` until Hocuspocus stores it, so that document is read every tick for those seconds. Counters: `reconcileStats {reads, skipped}`. **Projection hardening that the gate relies on (`tree.ts`):** a subscribe socket that answered a ping and then delivers nothing for two ping intervals (`TREE_PING_MS`, 30 s) is closed (→ not live → reads every tick, reconnect + snapshot); a vault that NEVER answered a ping is not judged by its silence (no reconnect loop, each reconnect is a full lean snapshot); `upsert`/`remove` frames arriving before a (re)connect snapshot completes are replayed onto the snapshot's rows; row stamps are compared as instants (`stampOlder`), not strings. Residual: a socket that is open but silently not delivering frames — on a vault that never pongs, or within the ~60 s before the watchdog fires — delays an external edit's fold up to the re-read interval — and a store in that window still re-reads the note itself (compare-and-set), so nothing is overwritten.
- **Hocuspocus unloads BY NAME** (`unloadDocument(doc)` only checks that *some* document is registered under `doc.name`, then deletes that entry). A late unload of an already-gone document (the `setTimeout(0)` after a store, a released direct connection) used to unregister the NEWER document loaded under the same name — an orphan people kept editing, a second copy for the next open, and a leaked Awareness interval (the intermittent `human-collab.test.ts` hang). `collab.ts` wraps `hocuspocus.unloadDocument`: wait for an unload in progress under that name, then unload only if `documents.get(name) === doc`. Keep the wrapper; test "a late unload of an already-unloaded document…".
- **Excalidraw**: paint RAW `Y.Map` values via `updateScene`; do NOT use `restoreElements` (it silently drops valid elements when reconciling fractional indices across a set). Loop-safety: `onChange` only writes elements whose monotonic `version` increased; the map observer ignores `LOCAL`-origin transactions. `appState` (zoom/scroll) is per-viewer, not synced.
- The Y.Map (canvas) re-seeds **idempotently** (set-by-id); the Y.Array (spreadsheet) can **double** if re-seeded while a client holds rows — only happens if the SQLite docState is lost under a live client.
- Test on throwaway `_test` notes; mint an edit capability against the running DB+secret (`createCapability` + `signCapability`) to drive `/collab/:id?t=` without owner login. Bust the PWA service worker (`getRegistrations().unregister()` + `caches.delete()`) or you test stale JS.

## Mentions, inbox, notifications, reminders, access requests (wave 2A)

- **Mention chip** = inline atom node `mention` in the SHARED schema (`lib/tiptap/MentionNode.ts`, registered in `collabExtensions()` AND the plain `DocumentRenderer`; attrs `kind` person|page|date, `id`, `label` (person name only), `date`, `reminder`, `uid`). HTML `<span data-type="mention" data-kind data-id data-mention-uid …>`. **A page chip stores NO title** — the browser node view (`MentionView`, installed via `setMentionNodeView`) resolves it through the reader's own permissions; unviewable → "No access", trashed → "Deleted page". Pure parser `@prism/core/mentions` (`lib/tiptap/MentionParse.ts`: `extractMentions`, `newMentions` diff by uid, comment tokens `@[Name](person:<id>)`).
- **Server (`src/notifications.ts`, routes `src/routes/notifications.ts`, mounted in `api.ts` before the owner short-circuit).** Tables created by the module (not db.ts): `notifications` (per recipient × vault, dedupe-keyed), `notification_settings`, `notification_email_log`, `reminders`, `access_requests`, `comment_participants`. Producers: (1) `noteContentStored` — diff old vs new chips after a document is persisted: the collab store (`collab.ts` `setDocumentStoreListener`; editors = accounts whose sockets/commands changed the doc, from Hocuspocus `onChange` context `email`/`human`/`mcp`) and REST content writes (`restMentionHook` on `/notes` + `/notes/:id`, pre-reads the old content only when the new body has a chip; never changes the response); a NEW person chip → the person note's email identities that have an account; never an author; only recipients who can VIEW the note; author must be able to view the chip's target; new page/person chips → ONE CAS links-only write `mentions` (backlinks), `markReconciled` when live. (2) `commentsStored` — new Y `comments` items vs a baseline taken at doc load → `comment_reply` to earlier participants (actorId → email via `documentActorId`, plus `comment_participants` for live-editor items without actorId), `comment_mention` for tokens. (3) Reminders worker. (4) `notifyShare` (acl note-people PUT), access-request outcomes. View is re-checked at creation, at delivery and at READ time (an item about a page you can no longer view disappears with its title; `access_denied` shows with `title: null`). Note refs come from the live tree projection (`noteInfo`), 15 s cache.
- **API.** `GET /api/notifications?box=inbox|archived&type=<mention|comment|reminder|access|exact type>&limit≤100&before=<at:id>` → `{items, next, unread}` · `GET /api/notifications/unread` · `POST /api/notifications/read {ids≤200 | all:true}` · `POST /api/notifications/archive {ids, archived}` · `GET/PUT /api/notifications/settings {settings:{mention|comment|reminder|access:{push,email}}}` → `+available{webPush,apns,email}` · `GET /api/reminders` · `POST /api/reminders {noteId, at, tz (IANA), uid?, dateOnly?}` (date-only fires 09:00 in `tz`, DST-correct; instants need an offset; ≤5 y; view required → else 404; 500 live per user) · `PATCH|DELETE /api/reminders/:id` (own only → else 404) · `POST /api/access-requests {noteId, level view|comment|suggest|edit, message≤500}` → **always 202** (lookup runs after the response; silently dropped for missing/trashed notes or access already held; deciders re-pinged at most daily; 20/h per user) · `GET /api/access-requests` (pending ones the caller may decide: workspace owner/admin, or view+`share` on the note) · `POST /api/access-requests/:id {decision, level?}` (a share-holder can't grant above their own caps → 403 `escalation`; approve = `upsertGrant` user/note; 409 `already_decided`). Signed-in users only (links/anon 401); every write `csrfRefusal` (DELETE: origin checks only); 16 KB bodies; per-user rate limits (`NOTIFY_READS_PER_MINUTE` 240).
- **Delivery.** `deliver()` → web push `{type:"notification", id}` + APNs `notificationAlert(id)` (generic "You have a new notification", `url: /inbox/<id>`) when the category's push setting is on; ids only. Email: `runEmailDigestOnce` sends ONE content-free "You have N unread notifications" per user per `NOTIFY_EMAIL_MIN_INTERVAL_MS` (6 h) for items unread after `NOTIFY_EMAIL_AFTER_MS` (30 min). Worker `startNotificationWorker` (index.ts; `NOTIFY_TICK_MS` 30 s, 0 = off): reminders (claim `scheduled→fired` = idempotent, batch 50, view re-checked) + digest + 90-day retention. **`/api/push/*` is now open to every signed-in user** (their own browser/device rows; agent-turn pushes still go to the session owner only) — 🔒 an endpoint is a URL the server POSTs to, so `isPushEndpoint` (push.ts) allows only real push services (`fcm.googleapis.com`, `android.googleapis.com`, `updates.push.services.mozilla.com`, `web.push.apple.com`, `*.push.apple.com`, `*.notify.windows.com`) on top of the media netguard URL rules (https:443, no IP literals/userinfo/private names), re-checked at send time; ≤10 rows per user (oldest pruned); an endpoint bound to another account → 409; subscribe/unsubscribe/test are CSRF-guarded; `/test` is 5/min and only the server owner gets per-endpoint counts. Budgets: per sender→recipient 20/h and per (sender, page, recipient) 8/h for mentions/comments (spent only after a non-duplicate insert); the recipient's 200/h budget only skips the push. Comment authorship = the batch's editor (an item's `actorId` is believed only if that account wrote in the batch); a REST autosave reads nothing when its chips are all in the last-seen chip cache. `push-sw.js` click → `/inbox/<id>` or `postMessage {type:"prism:open-notification"}`.
- **Client.** `lib/notifications/client.ts` (`notificationsApi` over `serverFetch`; desktop has no server → features hide), hooks + `anchor.ts` (open the page, scroll to `[data-mention-uid]`/`[data-reminder]`/`[data-comment-id]`). UI: `components/inbox/*` (virtual tab `notifications`, label Inbox), `components/home/*` (virtual tab `home`), Navigation Home/Inbox rows with unread badge, `@` menu (`MentionSuggest`/`MentionMenu`; people are looked up for a 120 ms-DEBOUNCED query — until it catches up the menu offers only people whose name fits the text typed NOW, never the older query's rows, which Enter used to take in place of a just-typed date: `parity2-mentions.spec.ts`), comment-composer mentions (`MentionText`). Tests: `test/notifications.test.ts`; fixtures `notion-mentions`, `notion-inbox`, `notion-home`, `notion-sharing`. Suggestion accepted/declined notifications: see *Wave 3 gaps* (driven by the mark's actor id, not the pending_suggestions row).

- **Assignment notifications (NP-CO-16) + per-page level (NP-CO-04).** *Assigned:* type `assigned`, settings category `assignment` (push + email ON by default; additive — `sanitizeSettings` starts from `DEFAULT_SETTINGS`, so a stored row without the key reads as the default and an older client's PUT keeps it; the client offers the row only when the server sent it). Producer `assignmentsStored` (`notifications.ts`) is called ONLY by `notifyAssignments` (`routes/databases.ts`), from two places: `writeProperties` (`POST /api/properties/:id` + `/batch`) and `restAssignmentHook` (`routes/notifications.ts`, mounted in `api.ts` beside `restMentionHook`, before the owner short-circuit: a metadata PATCH/PUT on `/notes/:id` — owner passthrough, member route and the Prism MCP's in-process dispatch; `mayAssignPeople` decides synchronously — assignee key, people-named key or a key some tag HINTS as a person; no tree, no vault call — so an icon/cover/status write costs nothing; only then ONE lean read (`getNote(id, {includeContent:false, includeMetadata})`, 5 s) gives the previous value, tags and path; a failed read or > 120/min per user = that write is not diffed). **Deliberately silent:** creates (`POST /notes`: template use, Duplicate, import rows), the CSV import, and every ingester/worker (they write through `vaultClient`, never these routes — `test/notifications-assign.test.ts` pins the caller list). *Which keys:* `personPropertyKeys` = the four task keys `assigned`/`assignee`/`assigneeEmail`/`assignee_email`, or a key the UI presents as a person (`inferKind` over vault type + schema-ui hint; the schema is read only for a people-named or hinted key; hints come from a per-vault cache dropped by `PUT /schemas/:tag`, TTL 30 s). *Who:* people ADDED — previous and new value are both resolved to accounts by ONE `assigneeResolver` (`my-tasks.ts`, the inverse of `assignedToMe`; one people-listing snapshot for both sides, index memoised on the cached listing; listing unreadable → `null` → NOTHING is diffed; lists compared whole up to 200 values, only the number notified per write is capped at 25: an address, a `[[wikilink]]`/path/id of a live human person note, a person note's name when exactly ONE note has it, the owner's configured person/addresses/aliases; never an account display name), so rewriting a name as a wikilink adds nobody. Never the author, only someone who can VIEW the page (creation, delivery, read), never a template (tag `template`, or a database row template = a page directly inside a folder named `Templates`); removed + re-added within an hour = one item (look-back over the reader's own rows for that page + property; the hourly dedupe key only guards a concurrent double write); one item per reader per write; in the per-sender budgets shared with mentions (`SPAMMABLE`); a share-link guest's write has no actor ("Someone") and is budgeted by `senderKey` = the link id; an MCP write reads "<name> (agent)" (a server-only `agent` flag in the stored anchor JSON, stripped from the wire anchor). Anchor `{property: <key>}` → `[data-property-key]` on the `PropertyBar` row (`REVEAL_PROPERTY_EVENT` un-folds it). *Per-page level:* table `notification_page_levels (email, vault_id, note_id, level)`, default `mentions` = no row; `GET/PUT /api/notifications/pages/:id {level: all|mentions|none}` (signed-in users; view required, else 404; PUT `csrfRefusal`, strict body). Enforced in `commentsStored`: `none` drops `comment_reply` for that reader (replies in page threads included; a mention of them, an assignment and suggestion outcomes still arrive); `all` adds type `comment_thread` (category `comment`) for a thread's FIRST item and every item of a `page: true` thread, to followers who are not the author, a mentioned person or a participant — same dedupe key as the reply, so one item per comment. **A comment batch with no account author** (a share-link guest's command runs as `capability:<id>`, which is not an editor) is budgeted as ONE sender per page (`senderKey: doc:<noteId>`) for reply, mention and thread items — never unbudgeted. Followers are read oldest-first (≤ 200). UI: page ⋯ "Notify me: …" group (`usePageMenuItems`; fetched only while the menu is open — pass `open`), `PageNotificationLevelButton` in the `CommentsSidebar` header (optional `noteId` prop; without it the control takes the page in front inside `#workspace-document`, or `/collab/<id>` on the share route, and hides inside a row peek) — both hide when the route answers 401/404 (share-link guests, older servers). Inbox: "Assigned" filter chip, an empty state per filter, **Shift+A** = Mark all read (only with focus inside the Inbox, never in a field). A purged page's level rows go with it: `onNotePurged` (`db.ts`) listeners run inside `deleteCollabSetAsideForNote`, i.e. at every existing purge hook (Trash purge, `DELETE /trash/:id`, member DELETE, the unsaved sweep; the owner passthrough DELETE only when the vault holds set-aside rows — its existing gate). Not done: a level for a page SUBTREE. Tests: `test/notifications-assign.test.ts`, `notion-assign.spec.ts`.

## Content Type & Renderer Pipeline

The full chain from vault note → screen:

1. `Canvas.tsx` fetches the active tab's note via `useNote(id)`
2. `inferContentType(note)` checks (in order): `metadata.prism_type` → `metadata.type` (if in `KNOWN_TYPES`) → tag mapping in `TAG_TO_CONTENT_TYPE` → path extension → default `"document"`
3. `getRenderer(contentType)` in `Registry.ts` returns the lazy-loaded React component
4. The renderer receives `RendererProps`: `{ note, onSave, onMetadataChange }`

**Virtual tabs** (e.g. `"messages-dashboard"`, `"calendar-dashboard"`, `"agent-activity"`) never hit Parachute — `Canvas.tsx` synthesizes a fake `Note` object and bypasses `useNote()`. Their `noteId` either contains `:` (non-numeric) or is in `VIRTUAL_TAB_IDS`.

**Adding a new renderer:**
1. Create `src/components/renderers/FooRenderer.tsx` — `export default function FooRenderer({ note, onSave, onMetadataChange }: RendererProps)`
2. Add lazy import and map entry in `Registry.ts`
3. Add `"foo"` to `ContentType` union in `src/lib/types.ts`
4. Add to `KNOWN_TYPES` in `content-types.ts` and to `TAG_TO_CONTENT_TYPE` if tag-driven
5. Add to `KNOWN_TYPES` list in the Rust `enrich_note` function

## State Management

Two Zustand stores, no cross-store dependencies:

- **`useUIStore`** (`src/app/stores/ui.ts`) — ephemeral UI: open tabs, active tab, sidebar/context-panel widths, command bar, inline prompt, ghost text, pending editor edits. Tab IDs are `"tab-{noteId}"`.
- **`useSettingsStore`** (`src/app/stores/settings.ts`) — persisted to `localStorage` as `"prism-settings"`: theme, fonts, vault URLs, AI provider/model routing (`skillModels`), sync defaults.

TanStack Query handles all server state. Query keys are defined in `src/lib/parachute/queries.ts`. Always invalidate via `queryKeys.vault.*` helpers, not raw strings.

## Tauri Commands & IPC

All commands are registered in `src-tauri/src/lib.rs` `invoke_handler`. The frontend wrapper lives in `src/lib/parachute/client.ts` (vault/system/GitHub) or `src/lib/agent/client.ts` (agent ops).

**Adding a Tauri command:**
1. Add `#[tauri::command] pub async fn my_cmd(...) -> Result<T, PrismError>` in the relevant `src-tauri/src/commands/*.rs` file
2. Add `commands::my_module::my_cmd` to `invoke_handler` in `lib.rs`
3. Add `invoke<T>("my_cmd", { ...params })` wrapper in the appropriate frontend client

Managed state injected into commands via `State<'_, T>`: `ParachuteClient`, `MatrixClient`, `GoogleClient`, `ModelRouter`, `AgentSessions`, `AppConfig`, `ServiceManager`, `DispatchManager`, `GitHubSyncState`, `NotionDbSyncState`.

## Background Services

`ServiceManager` (in `src-tauri/src/services/mod.rs`) starts tokio tasks on app launch — each runs a poll loop with a `watch::Receiver<bool>` shutdown channel:

| Service | Interval | Trigger condition |
|---|---|---|
| `message_sync` | 60s | `matrix_access_token` configured |
| `calendar_sync` | 5 min | `google_account_primary` configured; `disable_calendar_sync` stops it (server port: `CALENDAR_SYNC_ENABLED`, see Calendar ingest) |
| `email_sync` | 3 min | `google_account_primary` configured (server port: `GMAIL_SYNC_ENABLED`, see Gmail ingest) |
| `transcript_sync` | 10 min | `fathom_api_key` or `fireflies_api_key` configured (Meetily ingest + the idle `notion_task_sync` loop were **retired in WP1.4**) |
| `skill_scheduler` | varies | started via `start_scheduler()` after `DispatchManager` is ready; skips skill notes leased `runner: "server"`. **Superseded by the server skill scheduler** (below) once cut over |

**Ingest switch (WP0.4).** Which services start is decided by the pure `plan_services(config)` in `services/mod.rs` (unit-tested); `ServiceManager::start` / `start_scheduler` just act on it, and `get_service_status` overlays `disabled` + `disabled_reason` so the UI tells the truth. `ingest_mode: "host"` (default) | `"client"` in `prism-config.json`: **client starts no services and no skill scheduler** (one log line says so) so a laptop can run as a pure viewer/editor while the Prism Server ingests. Per-service opt-outs (all default false, all `#[serde(default)]`, restart required): `disable_message_sync`, `disable_fathom_sync`, `disable_fireflies_sync`, `disable_email_sync`, `disable_calendar_sync`, `disable_embedding_index`, `disable_skill_scheduler` (`disable_meetily_sync` / `disable_notion_task_sync` / `meetily_db_path` remain in the config struct as inert, retired fields so old files load). Settings -> Services -> "Ingest mode" (desktop only) edits the mode and the remaining flags; in Client mode the rows read "Runs on the Prism Server". **Client mode (WP1.4):** the desktop's `calendar_sync_range` (Calendar view) delegates to `POST /api/calendar/sync` through the `api_request` proxy whenever the server owns calendar (client mode or `disable_calendar_sync`); the proxy allowlist is exactly `/integrations[/…]` plus `POST /calendar/sync?from=YYYY-MM-DD&to=YYYY-MM-DD` (`api_request_allowed`, tested against tab/newline/`%2e`/dot-segment escapes). `scripts/check-desktop-independence.sh` (read-only; `PRISM_OWNER_TOKEN` env) is the M1 check: every active `/acl/workers` source must be kind=server + ok before quitting Prism.app. Full inventory: `docs/roadmap/architecture-v2/desktop-services-inventory.md`. `ensure_default_skills` is now **create-only**: it seeds missing default `agent-skill` notes but never rewrites an existing note's prompt or metadata (vault notes are the source of truth). Never run two ingesters for the same source: turn the desktop flag on before enabling the server worker.

`DispatchManager` (`src-tauri/src/services/agent_dispatch.rs`) handles on-demand background agent dispatches — each spawns a hardened `claude -p` process (`ClaudeUse::Dispatch`, see *Desktop agent runner*), tracks status, and optionally writes output to a Parachute note.

### Server skill scheduler (`apps/server/src/worker/skills.ts`, Arch v2 WP1.1)

The port of `skill_scheduler.rs` + `structured_skill.rs` + `DispatchManager` routing/persistence, run on the server worker tick (60 s, same as the desktop) so background skills (today `message-classify` + `clickup-task-triage`, structured, LM Studio) no longer need `Prism.app`. **`SKILLS_ENABLED=false` by default — then nothing is read, run or written.** `agent-skill` notes stay the source of truth (`skillName`, `enabled`, `intervalSecs`, `runAtHour`, `dependsOn`, `lastRun`, `executionMode`, `provider`/`model`, `structured`, prompt = content). The pass is fire-and-forget with an in-flight guard (a long classify never holds up ingest) and runs on the **primary vault only**.
- **Preserved exactly** (pinned by `test/skills.test.ts`): due = daily (`intervalSecs>=86400` + `runAtHour`) past the LOCAL hour and not yet run on the LOCAL date, else `now-lastRun >= intervalSecs` (never run / unparseable-RFC3339 lastRun → due); `dependsOn` = the named skill has a lastRun on today's local date, judged on the pass snapshot; `{{today}}/{{yesterday}}` (UTC) + `{{now}}`; `effective_routing` (note `provider`/`model` override `SKILLS_DEFAULT_PROVIDER`/`SKILLS_LOCAL_MODEL`; local = `local`|`ollama` + a model); lastRun = pass time written when a run is ACCEPTED (not after it finishes); structured parse/apply (union+dedup sourceTags, excludeTags + `triage-failed` skipped, `shortcutLabels`, URL stripping, 2500→800-char truncated retry, allowlist, `alsoAddTags`, same summary text; misconfig = failed dispatch); structured-without-local → claude with the desktop's rubric fallback prompt; `agent-dispatch`+`agent-output` notes at `vault/agent/dispatches/<date>/<slug>-<id8>` with the desktop content/metadata (+ `runner: "server"`).
- **Intentional changes:** agentic skills routed "local" run on the **WP0.1 claude runner** (the local MCP tool loop was not ported — it was the desktop's own fallback); a **local admission guard** before every local run (LM Studio reachable via `/api/v0/models`; runner thresholds (see Capacity: `AGENT_SWAP_MIN_FREE_MB`, `AGENT_SWAP_MAX_PCT`/`AGENT_FREE_MIN_PCT`; a model that is not already loaded — or whose state is unknown — is never JIT-loaded below `SKILLS_LOAD_FREE_MIN_PCT`=35 % free, the lms-guard threshold — nor (2026-10-03 stall, `jitLoadRefusal`; one rule behind `admitLocal` for the scheduler AND interactive local AI) when (a) memory free now < model size × `SKILLS_LOAD_HEADROOM` (1.0) + `SKILLS_LOAD_RESERVE_MB` (2048), with NO credit for swap on macOS — "free now" = `memory_pressure` free % × RAM, or with `SKILLS_LOAD_HEADROOM_SOURCE=reclaimable` vm_stat's free + cache pages (stricter; opt-in); model size = the model server's `size_bytes` if sent, else `SKILLS_LOCAL_MODEL_MB` (7168); (b) macOS is already swapping OUT faster than `SKILLS_LOAD_MAX_SWAPOUT_PER_S` (2000 pages/s; 0 = off) — a RATE from two `vm_stat` samples kept by the prober (`swapoutRate`); when the last sample is missing or older than 120 s `admitLocal` probes again after `SKILLS_LOAD_PAGING_WINDOW_MS` (2 s); (c) swap used > `SKILLS_LOAD_MAX_SWAP_USED_PCT` — unset = OFF on macOS (the OS resizes swap; 80–95 % used is normal), 80 on Linux. A resident model is never refused by these; a quantity the probe does not report skips its rule. **HONEST LIMIT: every signal is read BEFORE the load. The state measured on 2026-10-03 (60–80 % "free", swap 80 % used, nothing swapping) is ADMITTED by the defaults — this guard does not prevent that stall. Keeping the model resident (no JIT load at all) does**; `reclaimable` would have deferred it (vm_stat read 4–6 GB against the 9.2 GB needed) at the price of the skill never running until the model is resident. A pass whose model LOAD was refused is a `skills` failure outcome (third in a row = `failing` with the reason; the next admitted run recovers it) — other refusals still record nothing) — refused → no lastRun, no dispatch note, retried next tick, logged once per reason; one local run at a time; memory re-checked between notes against the lower `SKILLS_RUN_FREE_MIN_PCT` (8 %, + the swap floor — a resident 12B model alone holds the 16 GB mini at ~13–20 % free, so the 15 % start floor stopped every run after a note or two, 2026-10-01) and `SKILLS_LOCAL_RUN_TIMEOUT_MS` (25 min) — an early stop is written into the summary; an **unreachable LM Studio mid-run aborts** instead of stamping every note `triage-failed`; skill-note metadata writes carry `if_updated_at` (409 → refetch + merge once) instead of `force`; a future lastRun is not due; default skills are not seeded server-side.
- **Lease + cutover.** While enabled the server stamps `metadata.runner: "server"` on every enabled skill it takes over (a note pinned `runner: "desktop"` is skipped); the desktop scheduler skips `runner: "server"` notes. `lastRun` is not a lock — **never run both schedulers**. Runbook (overseer): `disable_skill_scheduler=true` in the desktop `prism-config.json` → restart Prism.app → set `SKILLS_ENABLED=true` + `SKILLS_DEFAULT_PROVIDER=local` + `SKILLS_LOCAL_MODEL=<desktop's local_ai_model>` (+ `SKILLS_LOCAL_BASE_URL` if not `http://127.0.0.1:1234/v1`) in `apps/server/.env` → restart pm2 `prism-server` → watch one full cycle of both skills (dispatch notes with `runner: "server"`, `GET /acl/workers` `skills` = kind server/ok). Rollback: `SKILLS_ENABLED=false` → restart pm2 → remove `runner: "server"` from the skill notes (or leave it: desktop honors it, so it MUST be removed before re-enabling the desktop scheduler) → `disable_skill_scheduler=false` → restart the desktop.
- **Health:** with `SKILLS_ENABLED=true`, `skills` is a SERVER source (`runSkillsPass` → `recordSourceOutcome`): failed runs/listing errors count toward the streak; finished runs, accepted claude dispatches and idle passes are successes; refusal-only passes record nothing, so sustained memory pressure surfaces as `stale` (`WORKER_STALE_SKILLS_MS`), not a failure storm. Exception: a refused model LOAD (see the admission guard) is a failure outcome per pass.
- **See + cancel runs (parity A).** `listRunningSkills()` / `cancelSkillRun(name)` over an in-memory registry of in-flight runs (one per skill name), exposed as `GET /api/agent/skills/running` + `POST /api/agent/skills/:skillName/cancel` (server owner BY EMAIL — a vault-role owner gets 403 — plus the live-actions CSRF guard; 404 `not_running`). Local structured run: an `AbortController` is checked before every note AND passed to `LocalModel.structured(…, signal)`, so the in-flight LM Studio request is aborted (`lmStudioClient` turns it into `SkillCancelledError`, never a timeout → the note is NOT flagged `triage-failed`); the summary says "Stopped early … (cancelled by the owner)". Claude run: `ClaudeDispatcher` now returns `{id, cancel}` (`cancelDispatch` through the run queue). **Behaviour change:** a cancelled run IS persisted (`status: "cancelled"`, content "**Status:** Cancelled") — the desktop dropped them; health treats it as a success. lastRun stays as written at acceptance (not immediately due again). The one-local-run slot (`tryAcquireLocalModel()` → an ownership token | null, `releaseLocalModel(token)` frees only the holder's slot) is shared with interactive local AI; both callers acquire AFTER their async admission check and defer/refuse when it is taken (review M1). The registry is keyed by RUN id, so several claude runs of one skill are all listed and all cancelled; a run with no cancel handle → 409 `not_cancellable` (L7). UI: AgentActivity "Running on the server" + Stop.
- **Skill config from a thin client (parity A).** No server route: AgentActivity's config card + builder write the skill note through the VaultClient (`vaultOps.updateSkillNote` / `validateSkillPatch` / `validateStructuredBlock`, mirroring the desktop card + `parseStructuredConfig`); the patch is merged onto the CURRENT metadata so `runner` + `lastRun` survive; `if_updated_at`, a metadata-only 409 refetched + merged once.

## AI / Model Routing

`ModelRouter` (`src-tauri/src/clients/model_router.rs`) routes agent calls to either:
- **Claude Code CLI** (default) — hardened `claude -p` subprocess (below); chat keeps continuity via `--session-id`/`--resume`
- **Ollama + MCP** (opt-in per skill) — `OllamaAgent` connects to `http://localhost:11434` with the Parachute MCP client built in

Per-skill routing is stored in `ModelRouter.skill_config` (a `Mutex<HashMap<String, SkillModelConfig>>`). Skills: `"edit"`, `"chat"`, `"transform"`, `"generate"`. Frontend controls routing via `ollama_cmds::set_skill_model`.

The `PRISM_CONTEXT` constant in `commands/agent.rs` is the system prompt prepended to all agent calls — it describes the vault MCP tools and Benjamin's data context.

### Desktop agent runner (`src-tauri/src/clients/claude_args.rs`, Arch v2 WP0.1d)

The desktop agents read vault content ingested from email/Matrix/transcripts, so a prompt injection must not reach the host (it used to: `--dangerously-skip-permissions`, every built-in tool, cwd = the repo root with its `CLAUDE.md`/`.mcp.json`/`apps/server/.env`/`prism-server.db`, the full inherited env). Every spawn — `ClaudeClient::run` / `run_conversational` (`clients/anthropic.rs`) and `run_claude_dispatch` (`services/agent_dispatch.rs`) — now goes through `build_claude_args` + `ClaudeRunner::command`; asserted by the Rust tests in both files.
- **argv** = `-p --model <m> --output-format text|json <persistence> --strict-mcp-config --mcp-config <per-run file> --tools "" --allowedTools <list> --permission-mode dontAsk --permission-prompts none --setting-sources "" -- <prompt>`. The model id is validated (an unsafe one → `sonnet`); the prompt is always after `--`.
- **Allowlists (`ClaudeUse`)**: `Edit`/`Transform`/`Generate` = read-only `query-notes, list-tags, find-path, vault-info, doctor` (they only RETURN text — the editor/CommandBar applies or saves it; `claude_use_for_skill`, unknown skill → read-only). `Chat` and `Dispatch` (background skills, custom tasks, the structured-skill claude fallback) = `query-notes, create-note, update-note, list-tags, find-path, vault-info, doctor, read-attachment, request-attachment-download` — never `delete-note`, never the admin verbs, never `request-attachment-upload`. All `mcp__parachute-vault__*`.
- **MCP config**: `RunMcpConfig` writes `$TMPDIR/prism-agent-<uuid>/mcp.json` (dir 0700, file 0600, both exclusive) holding ONLY `parachute-vault`, built from the `parachute-vault` entry of the managed `prism-mcp.json` (rewritten from the active vault at launch + on every vault switch; nothing else in it is copied). It is an RAII guard — deleted on exit, error, timeout, cancel, spawn failure. No managed config / no token → the run fails with a Settings hint (the repo `.mcp.json` fallback is gone).
- **cwd**: `<data_dir>/prism/agent-cwd` (macOS `~/Library/Application Support/prism/agent-cwd`), created 0700; a non-empty cwd refuses to run. Chat sessions persist under `~/.claude/projects/<slug of that cwd>/` (not in the cwd); `--resume` only works from the same cwd, so moving it orphans sessions (`AgentSessions` is in-memory anyway).
- **env**: `env_clear()` then HOME, USER, LOGNAME, LANG, LC_*, TMPDIR, TZ + a FIXED PATH (claude's dir, the resolved `node` dir for npm installs, `~/.local/bin`, `~/.npm-global/bin`, Homebrew, `/usr/local/bin`, `/usr/bin`, `/bin`) + `CLAUDE_STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS=300000`, `DISABLE_AUTOUPDATER=1`, `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_AUTO_MEMORY=1`. Auth = the CLI's own claude.ai login via HOME/USER (keychain); the desktop never passed an API key to the CLI and `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` from a dev shell is no longer inherited.
- **Cancel** (`agent_cancel_dispatch`) now really kills a `claude -p` dispatch (`kill_on_drop` + a per-dispatch `Notify`); a local-model run is still only marked cancelled.
- **Lost capability (deliberate):** no built-in `Read`, so the CLI can't read the temp file it spills an oversized MCP result to — skills must keep queries narrow (same trade-off as the server runner). No desktop feature needs Bash/Write/WebFetch.

### Server agent runner (`apps/server/src/agent-exec.ts`, `/api/agent/*`)

**SERVER-OWNER-only** (decision D3, "owner-only until per-actor tokens": `actor.kind === "user" && actor.role === "owner"` — an owner session or owner device token; admins/members/guests/links/anon → 403, because the run uses the VAULT token, which bypasses per-note grants and private-note rules) one-shot `claude -p` dispatches against the actor's active vault (hardened in Arch v2 WP0.1). The argv is a fixed template; the client supplies only prompt/skill/noteId. **Isolation flags** (all asserted by `test/agent-exec.test.ts`):
- `--strict-mcp-config --mcp-config <tmp>` — the ONLY MCP server is `parachute-vault` for the target vault, written per dispatch as a 0600 file in a 0700 `mkdtemp` dir and deleted when the run ends (exit, error, cancel, spawn failure). Without `--strict-mcp-config`, user-scope servers in `~/.claude.json` and the repo `.mcp.json` load too (`--mcp-config` only *adds*).
- `--tools ""` (no built-in tools at all — no Read/Write/Bash/WebFetch/WebSearch/Task) + `--allowedTools mcp__parachute-vault` + `--permission-mode dontAsk --permission-prompts none` — the vault MCP is auto-approved, everything else denied, nobody is prompted. `--dangerously-skip-permissions` is gone.
- `--setting-sources ""`, `--no-session-persistence` (one-shot only — sessions use `--session-id`/`--resume`, below), cwd = fixed EMPTY `~/.prism/agent-cwd` (`AGENT_CWD`; created 0700 lazily at first run; a **non-empty cwd refuses to run** so a planted CLAUDE.md can't become context).
- Env is an **allowlist** (`ENV_ALLOWLIST`: HOME/USER/LOGNAME/LANG/LC_*/TMPDIR/TZ + a fixed PATH + `CLAUDE_STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`, `DISABLE_AUTOUPDATER`, `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_AUTO_MEMORY=1` — auto-memory would read `~/.claude/projects/<cwd-slug>/memory/`, a context source outside the checked-empty cwd; verified off via `init.memory_paths: null`). HOME is what the CLI needs to find the claude.ai subscription login. No server secret (`PARACHUTE_TOKEN`, `SESSION_SECRET`, …, nor `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`) is ever inherited — an API-key login would need an explicit, deliberate addition.
- `resolveClaude()`: `which claude` → `~/.local/bin/claude` (native installer) → `~/.npm-global/bin/claude` → bare `claude`.

**Capacity.** Global semaphore `AGENT_MAX_CONCURRENT` (default 1) + memory admission (**macOS:** `memory_pressure` free% >= `AGENT_FREE_MIN_PCT` (15) AND kernel `kern.memorystatus_vm_pressure_level` < 2 at admission (a local skill run already in flight, model resident, tolerates `warn` and stops only at `critical` — a 7 GB model on the 16 GB mini sits at warn while inferring; 2026-10-01 runs stopped at 1/243 otherwise) AND absolute free swap >= `AGENT_SWAP_MIN_FREE_MB` (512) — the swap floor applies ONLY when the swap volume `/System/Volumes/VM` has < 4 GB free, since macOS adds swap files on demand (2026-10-01: 466 MB "free" swap with 159 GB disk free stopped classify, a false alarm) — NOT swap-% of total, since macOS grows swap on demand and % used reads 80-95% while memory is healthy; **Linux:** swap used <= 80% + free%; `AGENT_SWAP_MAX_PCT` is now an optional extra guard, honoured on any OS only if explicitly set, default unset on macOS / 80 on Linux, so a prod stopgap like 97 is harmless; refusal reasons name the metric, e.g. "memory pressure: 12% free (< 15%)" / "swap nearly exhausted: 300 MB free (< 512 MB)"; macOS probe = `sysctl vm.swapusage` + `memory_pressure -Q` — **not** `os.freemem()`, which excludes cache and always reads low; Linux = `/proc/meminfo`). Excess or memory-refused dispatches sit in status `queued` with a `queuedReason` and are re-checked every `AGENT_ADMISSION_RETRY_MS` (15 s); only a full queue (`AGENT_MAX_QUEUE`, 20) is refused (503). An unreadable probe fails OPEN (the cap still holds). `GET /api/agent/runner` reports running/queued/last verdict. `AGENT_MAX_BUDGET_USD` adds `--max-budget-usd` (default **1.00 per process/turn**; `0` disables). 30-min wall clock (SIGTERM, SIGKILL after 10 s).

**SSE** (`GET /api/agent/stream/:id`): `snapshot` (full dispatch once) → `delta` `{text}` (new output only) / `status` (metadata, no output) → `end` (metadata + `outputLength`), then closes. Never re-send the accumulated output per chunk.

**Live check:** `node --import tsx scripts/verify-agent-exec.ts --env <sandbox.env>` — requires an explicit env file (`AGENT_VERIFY_VAULT_URL/_VAULT/_TOKEN`), refuses :1940/:8787, provisions nothing, and asserts from the CLI's `system/init` (via `src/agent-init-check.ts`) that the tool list is ONLY `mcp__parachute-vault__*`, the only MCP server is `parachute-vault`, permissionMode is `dontAsk`, and cwd is the agent cwd. Tests inject spawner/probe/cwd via `configureAgentRunner` — never let a test fall through to the real spawner or the real `~/.prism`.

**One run queue.** `enqueueRun(RunSpec)` in `agent-exec.ts` is the single semaphore + memory-admission queue + 30-min clock + per-run 0600 MCP config for EVERY `claude` process — one-shot dispatches (`startDispatch`, used by `/dispatch` and the skill scheduler) and session turns compete for the same slots.

**Interactive model routing (parity A, `src/local-ai.ts`).** The port of the desktop `ModelRouter` per-skill routing + `local_ai_list_models`/`test_local_ai`, all server-owner-by-email (+ CSRF on mutations): `GET /api/agent/models` (the SERVER's LM Studio at `SKILLS_LOCAL_BASE_URL`: `/api/v0/models` with type + loaded state, `/v1/models` fallback; claude CLI presence), `GET/PUT /api/agent/routing` (`edit|chat|transform|generate` → `{provider:"claude", model: sonnet|opus|haiku}` or `{provider:"local", model:<id>}`; SQLite settings key `agent-routing`; default claude/sonnet; partial PUT merges, every entry validated), `POST /api/agent/routing/test` (local: one 16-token completion behind the guard; claude: CLI presence only — never spends a turn). **Who honours it:** only the read-only one-shot `POST /dispatch {profile:"vault-ro", skill}` the client's inline AI uses (HostServices `agentText({skill})`: ⌘J = `edit`, transforms = `transform`). Routed local → `startExternalDispatch` (same dispatch registry, so poll/SSE/cancel are unchanged; cancel aborts the LM Studio request) running ONE `/chat/completions` call (no tools — the prompt already carries the note; a leading `<think>` block is stripped) behind `admitLocal` (reachable, memory, no JIT-load under `SKILLS_LOAD_FREE_MIN_PCT`) + the shared one-local-run slot; a refusal FAILS the dispatch — never a silent Claude fallback (a local choice may be a privacy choice). Routed claude → `startDispatch` with `--model <alias>` (`ArgOptions.model`, allowlisted; default still sonnet). A full-tools dispatch or a non-interactive skill name is never routed. **Agent chat sessions always run on Claude** (multi-turn `--resume` + tools; `chat` routing only affects a one-shot chat-style dispatch). Review lows: a NEWLY routed local model must be listed by LM Studio (unknown → 400, unreachable → 409 `local_unavailable`; L4); `/routing/test` is limited to 6/min per owner (L4); `/dispatch` prompts ≤ 120k chars, a local reply body ≤ 2 MB and its text capped at 200k chars (L5); the skill card refuses a `dependsOn` cycle (L6). Tests: `test/agent-parity.test.ts` (fake LM Studio via `setLocalAiFetchForTests`, never :1234).

### Durable agent sessions (`apps/server/src/agent-sessions.ts`, Arch v2 WP3.1)

Multi-turn conversations a phone can start, leave and reconnect to. Tables (`db.ts`): `agent_sessions` (id = the CLI session uuid, `vault_id`, `owner_email`, `profile`, `note_id`, `cli_session_id`, `status idle|running|archived`, `transcript_note_id`, `cost_usd`), `agent_turns` (`status queued|running|done|error|cancelled|interrupted`, pid, exit_code, error, cost_usd), `agent_events` (`(session_id, seq)` PK, JSON payload). A session is visible only to its creator in the vault it was created in (other users' → 404); the router-wide SERVER-OWNER gate applies to every session route too (admin/member/guest/capability/anon → 403).

**API** (`/api/agent`): `POST /sessions {title?, noteId?, profile?: "vault-ro"|"vault-rw"}` → `{sessionId}` · `GET /sessions?limit=&archived=1` · `GET /sessions/:id` → `{session, turns[+finalText, tools, touched]}` · `POST /sessions/:id/turns {prompt, noteId?}` → `{turnId, status}` (409 while a turn is queued/running or the session is archived; 503 queue full, rolled back) · `GET /sessions/:id/stream?after=N` (SSE) · `POST /turns/:id/cancel` · `DELETE /sessions/:id` (archive: cancels an active turn, keeps the vault transcript, deletes the CLI's on-disk transcript). `/dispatch*` stays as the one-shot alias.

**Per-turn argv** = the hardened template + `--output-format stream-json --verbose --include-partial-messages` + turn 1 `--session-id <uuid>` / later `--resume <uuid>` (server-minted uuid, validated) + `--allowedTools` per profile: `vault-rw` = an EXPLICIT list `query-notes, create-note, update-note, delete-note, list-tags, find-path, vault-info, doctor, read-attachment, request-attachment-download` (never the whole server: the admin-verb tools `update-tag/delete-tag/rename-tag/merge-tags/prune-schema/manage-token` and `request-attachment-upload` stay denied); `vault-ro` = `query-notes, list-tags, find-path, vault-info, doctor` (vault 0.7.9 manifest verb `read`; `doctor` is a read-only scan that never auto-fixes). vault-ro also runs on a per-turn **READ-scoped hub token** (`parachute auth mint-token --scope vault:<v>:read`, 3 h TTL, `sub agent-session:<id>`, revoked at turn end; mint failure fails the turn CLOSED with 503; `AGENT_RO_READ_TOKEN=0` disables this second layer) (comma-joined, one argv element). Write tools still appear in `init.tools` under vault-ro, but dontAsk denies them — verified live: the call comes back as an error `tool_result` and no `note_touched` is emitted. `buildClaudeArgs` refuses any allowlist entry that isn't the vault server/its tools.

**Event schema** (`agent-events.ts` `StreamNormalizer`, stored as `agent_events.payload`): `init {cliSessionId, model, tools, mcp}` · `text_delta {blockId, text}` (**live only, never persisted**) · `text {blockId, text}` (the coalesced block; deltas with the same `blockId` concatenate to it exactly) · `tool_use {id, name, input}` (input **redacted** — secret-named keys, JWT/Bearer/`pvt_`/`sk-`/hex-key values — and truncated) · `tool_result {toolUseId, ok, summary}` (≤300-char scrubbed summary, never raw output) · `note_touched {noteId, op}` (from a SUCCESSFUL `create/update/delete-note` result only) · `status {status, reason?}` · `result {ok, costUsd?, durationMs, numTurns?, error?}`. SSE: persisted events are `id: <seq>` / `event: <t>` / `data: {seq, turnId, …event}`; deltas are `event: text_delta` with **no id** (so `Last-Event-ID` only ever points at persisted seqs). Replay = seq > max(`?after`, `Last-Event-ID`), subscribe-before-replay with seq dedupe, then live until the turn's terminal `status`; with nothing in flight it closes after the replay. `: ping` every 25 s.

**Lifecycle.** Open-note content goes into the FIRST turn only (≤8000 chars, fenced as data); later turns get at most `Active note: <id>`. `index.ts` runs `bootSweepAgentSessions()` before serving: queued/running turns → `interrupted` (+ a persisted status event), sessions → `idle`. On every turn end the whole session re-renders into ONE vault note `vault/agent/sessions/<created-date>/<slug>-<id8>` (tags `agent-session` + `agent-dispatch`, metadata in the AgentActivity dispatch shape + `sessionId/profile/turns/costUsd`), holding prompts, final replies, tool NAMES and touched ids — never tool inputs/results. The note is **private to the session owner** (`metadata.prism_creator` = owner email, `prism_visibility: "private"`). Written to the session's own vault (`vaultClient(vault_id)`), best-effort; `AGENT_TRANSCRIPT_MIRROR=0` turns mirroring off. Never re-rendered for an archived session.

**Guards + retention.** Open-note context needs `effectiveCaps(...).has("view")` for the requesting actor (someone else's private note → 403, turn rolled back); internal callers with no actor never inline note content. `AGENT_SESSION_BUDGET_USD` (default 10; ≤0 off) refuses new turns once the session's cumulative `cost_usd` reaches it (409 `budget_exceeded`). **One active turn per user across sessions**: further turns wait FIFO as `queued` ("waiting for your other agent turn to finish") and start when the slot frees (the per-session rule stays a 409). `text`/`text_delta` pass through `scrubSecrets` (a token split across deltas is only caught in the final `text`). **Archive** cancels an active turn, deletes the CLI's `<id>.jsonl` AND sidecar dir `<id>/` (tool-results), and deletes the session's `agent_turns` + `agent_events` rows (the session row + transcript note stay); a child that outlives the archive purges again in its onEnd. `startAgentMaintenance()` (boot + daily): prunes events older than `AGENT_EVENTS_RETENTION_DAYS` (30) for live sessions, wipes rows left on archived sessions, and deletes uuid-named CLI artifacts under the agent-cwd project dir older than `AGENT_CLI_RETENTION_DAYS` (14) that don't belong to a non-archived session (non-uuid names like `memory/` are never touched). `seq` comes from `agent_sessions.event_seq` (a counter), so pruning never reuses a seq. `cliSessionFile`/`cliSessionSidecar` refuse non-uuid ids before any fs op.

**Session gotchas (verified against CLI 2.1.x, stub MCP).**
- **The CLI does NOT write into the cwd.** It persists the conversation at `$HOME/.claude/projects/<slug>/<uuid>.jsonl`, slug = `realpath(cwd)` with every non-alphanumeric → `-` (`cliSessionFile()`). The cwd stays empty across turns, so the WP0.1 emptiness guard is unchanged — but `--resume` finds the session **by cwd slug**, so every turn must run from the same fixed cwd (changing `AGENT_CWD` orphans existing sessions). That file (and a `<uuid>/` sidecar dir for large tool results) holds raw tool results (vault content), 0600; archive deletes both.
- `--session-id <id>` on an id the CLI already has fails ("Session ID … is already in use"); `--resume` of an unknown id fails ("No conversation found", `error_during_execution`, $0). So turn N resumes iff `cli_session_id` was recorded OR `cliSessionFile` exists (a turn 1 that died after the CLI wrote its file).
- `result.total_cost_usd` is **cumulative across `--resume`**; per-turn cost = delta vs `agent_sessions.cost_usd`. `--max-budget-usd` (`AGENT_MAX_BUDGET_USD`) is **per process/turn**, not per session — verified: a resume with a cap BELOW the already-accumulated session total still ran and succeeded. The session-level cap is therefore enforced server-side (`AGENT_SESSION_BUDGET_USD`).
- Fixtures `test/fixtures/agent-stream-*.jsonl` are real CLI output with host-specific init fields (skills, plugins, memory paths, sockets) and thinking signatures stripped — re-record the same way if the CLI's stream shape changes.

### Agent profiles, budgets, billing label (Arch v2 WP3.4; `agent-profiles.ts`, `agent-billing.ts`, docs `docs/agent-profiles.md`)

- **Profiles** (explicit `--allowedTools` lists, never a whole server; `buildClaudeArgs({server})` refuses any entry outside the chosen server — `vault` → `mcp__parachute-vault__*`, `prism` → `mcp__prism__prism_*`): `vault-ro` / `vault-rw` (as WP3.1), `skill` (= vault-rw minus `delete-note`; used by the WP1.1 claude-routed skill dispatches via `startDispatch(..., {allowedTools})`; never a session, never inlines a note; not creatable via the API), and — behind **`AGENT_PRISM_PROFILES=true`** (default off; `POST /sessions` → 400 and an existing prism session's turn → 409 `profile_unavailable` while off) — `prism-ro` / `prism-rw`. The prism profiles point the per-turn MCP config at THIS server's own `http://127.0.0.1:<PORT>/mcp` (server name `prism`) and carry NO vault token. `prism-ro` = the 12 read-scope tools; `prism-rw` = those + `prism_create_note/update_note/restore_version/add_comment/resolve_comment/suggest_edit/sheet_update`; deliberately never `prism_delete_note`, `prism_share`, `prism_propose_change/vote/withdraw_proposal`. `test/agent-profiles.test.ts` asserts every name exists in `PRISM_TOOLS` with the matching scope — add a Prism tool and decide its profile explicitly.
- **Per-turn credential (prism-*).** A `pp_` PAT minted by `issueInternalPat` (`auth/pat.ts`: label `agent-turn:<turnId>`, scope read|write by profile, bound to the session's vault + owner email, TTL ≤ 3 h, hash-only storage like every PAT) at **spawn time** through `RunSpec.mcpConfig()` — so a turn cancelled while queued or waiting on the user slot never mints one. It lives only in the 0600 per-run MCP config (never argv, never logged, never an event) and is revoked in `onEnd`, on rollback, on cancel, and by `bootSweepAgentSessions` (`revokeInternalPats`, for a crash mid-turn). It resolves to the owner's actor, so Prism's per-note grants/private-note rules/caps still bound what the agent can do (the profile allowlist is a second layer). Internal PATs are hidden from `GET /auth/pats`, don't count toward the 25-token cap, and a user label can't use the reserved `agent-turn:` prefix.
- **Budgets.** Per-turn `AGENT_MAX_BUDGET_USD` (1.00), per-session `AGENT_SESSION_BUDGET_USD` (10, 409 `budget_exceeded`) and NEW per-user per-day `AGENT_DAILY_BUDGET_USD` (**25**, ≤0 = off): refused at turn start with 409 `daily_budget_exceeded` once spend since LOCAL midnight reaches it (a running turn is not interrupted; the per-turn cap bounds overshoot). Spend is read from the `agent_cost_log` ledger (one row per finished turn with a cost, pruned after 60 d), **not** `agent_turns`, because archiving a session deletes its turn rows and must not reset the day. Budgets are server config; the UI shows them read-only.
- **Billing mode.** The runner uses the `claude` CLI's login. `agent-billing.ts` probes `claude auth status` once at boot (+ hourly), with the runner's env allowlist, reading ONLY `authMethod` (`claude.ai`/oauth → `subscription`, api key/bedrock/vertex → `api`, else `unknown`); injectable via `configureBilling` (tests never run the real CLI); reads never probe. On a subscription the CLI's `total_cost_usd` is an **API-equivalent estimate, not a charge**.
- **API.** `GET /api/agent/runner` now includes `billing`; `GET /api/agent/limits` → `{billing, session:{limitUsd}, daily:{limitUsd,spentUsd,remainingUsd,resetsAt}, profiles:[…selectable], defaultProfile:"vault-ro"}` (owner-only like all of `/api/agent`). Client: `AgentClient.getLimits?()`, `useAgentLimits()`, pure `lib/agent/cost.ts` (`formatAgentCost` → `≈$0.02 API-equiv.` + tooltip on subscription/unknown, plain `$0.02` on api). UI: AgentChat turn footer, session list, conversation header budget line (`AgentBudget.tsx`), the new-session profile picker (profiles from `limits.profiles`), AgentActivity session rows. `verify:agent` pins the wording.
- **Read-only proof.** `agent-init-check.ts` `toolsDeniedByProfile` / `checkReadOnlyEnforced` over the recorded `system/init` (`agent-stream-ro-denied.jsonl`: the CLI LISTS `create-note`/`update-note` even under vault-ro; the allowlist + dontAsk deny them, and the same fixture's denied `tool_result` touches no note).

### Agent client seam + chat UI (Arch v2 WP3.2)

**Seam.** `packages/core/src/lib/agent/sessions.ts` defines `AgentClient` (`createSession`, `listSessions`, `getSession`, `sendTurn`, `streamSession(id, afterSeq, handlers) → unsubscribe`, `cancelTurn`, `archiveSession`, optional `scope()`) + wire types copied from the server (`AgentSession`, `AgentTurn`, `AgentEvent`, `AgentStreamMessage`, `AgentApiError{status, code, detail, turnId}`). Injected like VaultClient: `<AgentClientProvider client>` / `useAgentClient()` (null = no provider) in `data/AgentClientContext.tsx`. `createHttpAgentClient({fetch, sse?, headers?, scope?})` (`lib/agent/httpAgentClient.ts`) is the transport-agnostic HTTP impl; **web** instantiates it in `apps/web/src/agent/HttpAgentClient.ts` with `serverFetch` + `streamServerSSE` + `contextHeaders()` (so PWA cookie and native bearer both work; sessions are per active vault) and provides it in `main.tsx` (null for capability viewers).
- **Desktop has NO AgentClient** (decision: keeps its Tauri `agent_chat` path until WP4). Its webview can't reach the server as owner: the Tauri CSP `connect-src` has no `http://localhost:8787`, the Rust `api_request` proxy is allowlisted to `/integrations` and can't stream SSE, and a public `wss://` collab origin fails the loopback-only COLLAB_TOKEN owner check. With no provider every entry point hides itself.
- **Visibility gate:** `useAgentAvailability()` probes `GET /sessions?limit=1` once (query key `["agent-available", scope]`, 10 min stale) → `yes | no (401/403/404) | error | checking | none`. `useAgentAvailable()` is the boolean every entry point reads, so admins/members (403) and offline never see it.

**Reducer (pure, `lib/agent/sessionReducer.ts`).** `seedConversation(detail)` builds finished turns from the detail summary (`finalText`/`tools`/`touched`) and RESETS an in-flight turn, returning `streamAfter = firstSeq - 1` (or the session `lastSeq` if the turn has no events; `null` = nothing in flight → no stream). `applyAgentMessage`: persisted events apply at most once (`seq <= lastSeq` ignored); `text_delta` appends to a `streaming` block by `blockId`; the persisted `text` REPLACES it (authoritative + secret-scrubbed); a delta for a final block is ignored; unknown turn → placeholder (the hook refetches). The server's `GET /sessions/:id` now returns per-turn `firstSeq`/`lastSeq` and a session `lastSeq` for this. **Test:** `npm run verify:agent -w @prism/web` (`apps/web/scripts/verify-agent-reducer.ts`, same pattern as `verify-sse.ts`): coalescing, blockId replacement, replay dedupe, drop+reconnect convergence, seed/rebuild, and `streamSessionEvents` against a loopback SSE server.

**Reconnect (`lib/agent/useAgentConversation.ts`).** Open = `getSession` → seed → stream `?after=streamAfter`; drops reconnect with `Last-Event-ID` (streamSSE backoff); the stream stops after the turn's terminal status (the server closes when idle; `streamSessionEvents` stops on the next CLEAN reconnect that delivers nothing — never on a reconnect after an error — so it doesn't poll). `visibilitychange`/`online` re-runs the open while a turn is in flight (phones kill sockets in the background). `sendTurn` 409 `conflict` (+`turnId`) → re-open = attach to the running turn; `budget_exceeded` / `busy` / 403 → friendly copy (`agentErrorText`).

**UI.** `components/agent/AgentChat.tsx` is the `"agent-chat"` virtual tab (Registry + Canvas `VIRTUAL_TAB_IDS` + Navigation `VIRTUAL_TABS` + ShareButton `VIRTUAL`): session list (title, last activity, running dot from `lastTurnStatus`, archive) + conversation (user bubble, streaming text, tool chips with ok/error, "Created/Updated/Deleted" note chips that open the note, `duration · cost` footer, Stop while running, `turnProblem` copy for error/cancelled/interrupted/budget). A new session is a **draft** until the first send (profile toggle Read-only default / Read-write; title = first 80 chars of the prompt). Mobile: the conversation is a `fixed` full-screen layer (z `--z-overlay`, above the command pill) sized to `visualViewport.height` so the composer sits above the keyboard, safe-area insets, 16px input. Nav state lives in `lib/agent/chatStore.ts` (`activeSessionId` remembered in localStorage `prism:agent-session`, `pendingAsk`, `openAgentChat({sessionId?, ask?})`, `isAskableNoteId`). Entry points (all behind `useAgentAvailable`): sidebar "Agent chat", command bar "Agent Chat" / "Ask About This Note" / the "Ask Claude: …" row (now wired: new session with that prompt), mobile More sheet "Agent chat" / "Ask about this note" (replaces "Ask the agent" there), context-panel `PanelChat` (→ compact `AgentPanelChat`, lazy), AgentActivity "Chat sessions" section + web Custom Task box (transcript notes tagged `agent-session` are then dropped from the dispatch list to avoid duplicates). Deep link: `/agent` and `/agent/<sessionId>` boot into the tab (client route — SW denylist unchanged; WP3.3's push link). "Ask about this note" continues the newest session whose `note_id` matches, else starts a draft bound to the note (first turn inlines it server-side).

### Agent on pages (NP-AI-03 / NP-AI-01) and locked pages (NP-PG-09)

- **Summarize / Draft / Transform a page or a selection** (`lib/agent/pageActions.ts` pure half + store, `components/agent/PageAgentPanel.tsx`, `PageAgentHost` mounted in `Shell`). Every action is ONE **text-only** run: `HostServices.agentText(prompt, {skill, textOnly: true, signal})` → `POST /api/agent/dispatch {profile:"text"}` — a client-requestable NARROWING like `vault-ro`, one step further: NO MCP server (`NO_MCP_CONFIG`, still `--strict-mcp-config`), no `--allowedTools`, no built-in tools, the prompt exactly as sent (no vault preamble, no `Active note`), `noteId` dropped; routed per skill (summarize/draft = `generate`, transform = `edit`; a local route has no tools either). So "Sources" — this page by title, the selection, characters read / of — is the WHOLE input ("The agent was given only this text"). **Release order: restart the server BEFORE the PWA ships** — an older server answers 400 to `profile:"text"`; the panel then says "Update the server to use page AI actions" with no retry (never a dead spinner). `scripts/verify-agent-exec.ts` has a text-only case (init tools empty, no MCP server) — NOT run yet. NOT verified against the real CLI: that `claude -p` accepts an MCP config with no servers and no `--allowedTools` (tests use the injected spawner).
