Entry points — page ⋯, command bar, the selection toolbar's "AI" menu, the block menu — exist only where `useHostServices()` is non-null (the server owner today) AND the page is open in a text editor (`pageAgentReady`; never on sheet/canvas/code/virtual tabs); offline they are disabled with the reason. Options are fixed lists (`validOption` — never free text in a prompt). **The result is a proposal:** the non-modal panel (phone: bottom sheet) shows the text, the original selection and Sources, then Replace selection / Insert below / at top / at cursor / at end / Copy / Discard. **Positions are tracked, never raw:** `requestPageAgent` registers the selection + caret and maps them through EVERY editor transaction (`track`, ProseMirror mapping; a remote Yjs edit is such a transaction) until the panel closes. Replace is offered only when `replaceRefusal` is empty: the mapped range still holds exactly the captured text AND contains no non-text leaf (image, mention, sub-page row, line break) and no link / comment / suggestion mark — otherwise the reason is shown and Insert below is offered. "cursor" / "below" resolve at apply time to the END of the top-level block (`afterBlock`) — never inside a block. A write is ONE editor transaction of text-only paragraph nodes. Copy only when the page is locked or the editor is not editable. Limits: page ≤ 60,000, selection ≤ 20,000 characters, with a visible note; a truncated selection cannot be replaced. Cancel / Esc / unmount abort the run; a failure is inline with "Nothing was changed" + Try again. **Prompt-injection hygiene:** title, page text and selection sit in `<page_title>` / `<page_text>` / `<selected_text>` blocks, and `defuseFenceTags` rewrites the `<` of ANYTHING in the content that reads as one of those tags — open or close, any case, blanks/newlines after `<`, around `/` and before `>` — to `‹` (linear scan). Fixture `notion-page-agent.html`, spec `notion-page-agent.spec.ts`; `npm run verify:host -w @prism/web` pins the `text` profile mapping.
- **Header Agent button (NP-AI-01):** `TabBar` mounts `HeaderSelectionAsk` (only inside a query client, with an agent client, for a page open in an editor) and the button calls the same `useSelectionAsk().ask("selection")` as the toolbar / block menu / slash / ⌘J when text is selected — an UNSENT attachment in the same document-bound conversation; nothing selected (or a capture already pending) → it opens the panel as before.
- **Locked pages and agents (NP-PG-09).** Lock = `metadata.prism_locked` (set only by `POST /api/notes/:id/meta`). **Every check FAILS CLOSED:** only a definite "unlocked" or a definite 404 lets a write through; a read error/timeout answers 503 `lock_unknown` (retryable). (1) **Session turns:** `startTurn` reads the lock at EVERY turn start (`agentNoteLock`) for the note the turn names, the note the session is bound to, every `contextNoteIds` entry and every snapshot's source page; on a profile that writes with the vault token (`bypassesPageLock`: `vault-rw`, `skill`) a locked one refuses the turn — 423 `{error:"locked"}` "This page is locked — unlock it or use a read-only session.", rolled back, nothing spawned (a queued follow-up is blocked with that text). `vault-ro` and the prism-* profiles run. (2) **One-shot `/dispatch` with a `noteId`** and no narrowing profile: 423 / 503 likewise; `vault-ro` and `text` run. (3) **Owner passthrough** (`ownerLockRefusal` in `routes/api.ts`): 423 for owners and admins on every way to change a locked page's BODY — PATCH/PUT with `content`/`append`/`prepend`, `POST /notes/:id/restore`, and `POST /notes` with `if_exists: replace|update` carrying content (single or `notes:[…]` batch; target by `id` or path; a refused batch writes nothing). Lock state from the tree projection (`treeLockState`), else one vault read. A batch's targets are resolved through the tree projection in one pass (`treeLockLookup`: ids and paths, case-insensitive/NFC), a vault read only for what it cannot answer (4 at a time), and a batch with more than `MAX_OVERWRITE_ITEMS` (500) overwriting items is refused 413 `batch_too_large` — never partly checked. Metadata/tags/path stay writable, unlock is `/meta`, and a body the gateway does not rewrite is still forwarded byte for byte. The client flushes pending saves before LOCKING and does NOT lock if that save fails or is still queued on the device for THIS page (`VaultClient.hasPendingWritesFor`, falling back to the audience-wide check; `usePageActions.toggleLock`). **What is NOT enforced — be honest about it:** a `vault-rw` agent can still write OTHER locked pages by id through the vault MCP (the vault has no lock concept, and the turn runs on the vault token). The complete fix is the `prism-rw` profile (behind `AGENT_PRISM_PROFILES`) — owner decision c.9 in `PARITY-GAPS.md`. Tests: `test/agent-sessions.test.ts` (NP-PG-09 …), `test/pages-review.test.ts` (LOCK: …).

## Configuration

Desktop config loads from the platform app-config dir — on **macOS that is `~/Library/Application Support/prism/prism-config.json`** (Tauri app-data), not `~/.config/prism`. On first launch the file is created with defaults. The Settings UI writes back to it. No external `.env` is required for desktop. (The web/server path uses `apps/server/.env` instead — see *Prism Server* above.)

Key fields in `AppConfig`:
- `parachute_url` (default: `http://localhost:1940`) — server root; the client appends the vault-scoped `/vault/{name}/api` path itself
- `parachute_vault` (default: `default`) — vault name for the scoped REST/MCP URLs (`/vault/{name}/...`)
- `parachute_api_key` — Bearer token for vault auth. As of Parachute **0.5.x** this is a **hub-issued JWT** (`vault:<name>:write`, ≤1yr TTL), minted via `parachute auth mint-token`. The pre-0.5 `pvt_*` opaque tokens are rejected (401). The hub (`@openparachute/hub`, port 1939) is the token issuer.
- `matrix_homeserver` / `matrix_access_token` / `matrix_user`
- `google_account_primary` — account name for `gog` CLI
- `notion_api_key`
- `fathom_api_key` — transcript source (`meetily_db_path` is retired/inert)
- `anthropic_api_key` — also checked in macOS Keychain as fallback

## Parachute MCP

`.mcp.json` at project root registers the Parachute vault MCP server at `http://127.0.0.1:1940/vault/default/mcp` (vault-scoped as of Parachute 0.5.x; the old unscoped `/mcp` was removed). Auth is the same hub JWT as the REST client. With a `write` token (Prism's), vault 0.7.9 exposes these tools (prefixed `mcp__parachute-vault__`); `update-tag`/`delete-tag`/`rename-tag`/`merge-tags`/`prune-schema`/`manage-token` need an **admin** token and are hidden (calling one returns `Unknown tool`):

- `query-notes` — read by ID/path, or filter by tag/search/path/metadata/date; supports `near` for graph neighborhoods
- `create-note` — single or batch (pass `notes` array)
- `update-note` — content, metadata merge, tags add/remove, links add/remove
- `delete-note`
- `list-tags` — with optional schema detail
- `update-tag` — upsert tag description + field schema (**admin only** on 0.7.x)
- `delete-tag` (**admin only** on 0.7.x)
- `find-path` — BFS shortest path between two notes
- `vault-info` — vault description + stats
- `doctor` — read-only integrity census (run after upgrades)
- `read-attachment`, `request-attachment-upload`, `request-attachment-download` — attachment bytes / single-use ticket URLs

**Tag-schema writes need `vault:<name>:admin`** (vault ≥0.7.1): REST `PUT/DELETE /tags/:name` → 403 `insufficient_scope` with a write token. Every seeder (`scripts/lib/seed-tag-schemas.ts`, `prism-setup.ts`, `commons-init.mjs`, `vault-provision.ts`, desktop `schema_seed.rs`) mints a 1h admin token via `parachute auth mint-token --scope vault:<name>:admin --ephemeral` (`mintEphemeralAdminToken` in `mcp-token.ts`; `PARACHUTE_ADMIN_TOKEN` overrides) and turns a 403 into an actionable error. Indexable field types on 0.7: string, integer, boolean, reference, date — `indexed:true` on number/array is a 400, and a wrong-typed write to an indexed field is a 422 (nothing written).

## Prism MCP (`/mcp`, Architecture v2 WP6.1)

The Prism Server serves its OWN MCP endpoint — distinct from the vault MCP above. It acts as the caller's **Prism account** (per-note grants, private notes, caps), so it is the safe way to give member agents access. User doc: `docs/mcp-access.md`.

- **Transport:** stateless Streamable HTTP, MCP 2026-07-28 (`@modelcontextprotocol/server` 2.x `createMcpHandler`, a fresh `McpServer` per request; 2025-era `initialize` clients served by the SDK's stateless fallback). Code in `apps/server/src/mcp/`: `router.ts` (`mountPrismMcp(app, mountPath)` — also mountable at `/surface/prism/api/mcp`; RFC 9728 metadata at `/.well-known/oauth-protected-resource<mount>`, Origin check, rate limits, 401 `WWW-Authenticate: Bearer resource_metadata=…`), `auth.ts` (bearer → `McpPrincipal`), `tools.ts` (registry), `dispatch.ts`, `errors.ts`, `tool-whoami.ts`.
- **Auth (bearer only — session cookies and capability links are NEVER accepted on /mcp):** `pp_` Prism PATs (table `mcp_pats`, `auth/pat.ts`; SHA-256 only, bound to ONE vault, `read|write` ceiling, 1–365 d; managed at `/auth/pats` via `routes/pats.ts` + Settings → Account → "Agent access tokens"); `pd_` device tokens; the owner's hub JWT — **OPT-IN** (off unless `MCP_OWNER_HUB_SUBS` is set; then it needs `vault:<primary>:admin` scope **AND** an exactly-allowlisted `sub`; member-minted `mcp:*`/`mcp_tokens` JWTs and `scoped_tags` tokens refused; verifier injectable via `setHubJwtVerifier`); `COLLAB_TOKEN` loopback-only. **A PAT is recognised only by `/mcp`** — `resolveActor()` doesn't know it, so it is never a web credential. Revoking a device revokes PATs minted through it.
- **Tools:** `defineTool({name, scope: "read"|"write" REQUIRED, description, inputSchema (zod/v4), annotations: {readOnlyHint REQUIRED, must agree with scope — else defineTool throws}, access REQUIRED, handler})`. `tools/list` = only tools whose `access(principal)` is true (read-only credentials additionally see only read-scope tools, and a write-scope tool is refused at CALL time too); `access(principal, args)` is re-checked per call; a hidden tool answers exactly like a nonexistent one. Handlers return JSON or throw `ToolError`; `mapToolError` maps everything else (vault errors like `vaultErr`; unknown → `internal_error`, logged, never echoed). One audit line per call, no args. Add tools to `PRISM_TOOLS` in `router.ts`.
- **Tools reach data through the gateway, not around it:** `ctx.dispatch(path, init)` runs a Prism route IN-PROCESS as the principal's actor (`app.request(..., env)` with the actor under the module-private `INPROCESS_ACTOR` symbol that `resolveActor` honours first; cookie/authorization stripped, `X-Prism-Vault` pinned). So `effectiveCaps`/anti-escalation/private notes apply unchanged. `dispatchAsActor(app, principal, path, init)` only accepts `/api/…` paths with no `.`/`..` segment (raw or %-encoded); a read-only principal may only dispatch GET/HEAD (+ `READ_ONLY_POST_ROUTES`, empty); dispatches carry `INPROCESS_CLIENT_KEY` so rate limiters key on `mcp:<via>:<credentialId>`, and a forwarding header so `isLocalRequest` is never true for them. `jsonOrToolError(res)` maps statuses. Only Yjs tools (WP6.3) need direct code.
- **Core note tools (WP6.2, `mcp/tool-notes.ts`):** `prism_query_notes`, `prism_get_note` (+`_caps`, `collab:{kind,live}`), `prism_semantic_search` (primary vault only; 409 → clear `invalid_request`), `prism_create_note`, `prism_update_note` (`if_updated_at` REQUIRED), `prism_delete_note` (destructiveHint), `prism_list_tags`, `prism_list_versions`, `prism_get_version`, `prism_restore_version`; resource `prism://note/{id}` (Markdown for document kind — converted in the conversion worker, raw-HTML fallback, see *Content conversion* — raw otherwise; `PrismResource` in `tools.ts`, `PRISM_RESOURCES` in `router.ts`). `access()` is only the early "does any grant give this cap anywhere" gate; the gateway decides per note. **Owner/admin requests hit the vault PASSTHROUGH, not the gateway handlers**, so the tools bridge the dialect: owner PATCH uses `tags:{add,remove}` (non-owner: `add_tags/remove_tags`), owner text search is `GET /api/notes?search=` (non-owner: `/api/search?q=`), non-owner lists ignore `tag/path_prefix/limit` (applied in the tool), and owner responses carry no `_caps` (computed with `effectiveCaps`). **Live docs:** `collab.ts` `isDocLive(vaultId, noteId)` (= Hocuspocus has the doc loaded) / `hasLiveState` (loaded, or holding live changes that have not reached the vault); restores are refused with `conflict` while either holds, content writes MERGE through Yjs (WP6.3, below) — the view gate (GET) runs first so liveness is never an oracle. 409s are classified by `errors.ts` `conflictError` (vault `error_type`: stale `conflict` vs `path_conflict` → "a note already exists at <path>", `ambiguous_path`, …); only a stale-token conflict gets the "re-read and retry" hint (`isStaleConflict`). Outputs are bounded (lists ≤ 200 rows, preview 2,000 chars; single note 100,000). Tests: `test/mcp-tools.test.ts` (21; the `mcp-endpoint.test.ts` tool-list tests now filter to whoami).
- **Collab-safe tools (WP6.3, `mcp/tool-collab.ts` + pure Yjs ops in `src/collab-ops.ts`):** `prism_list_comments`, `prism_add_comment` (`quote` → new thread anchored by a `comment` mark on the first match within one paragraph, or `thread_id` → reply), `prism_resolve_comment`, `prism_suggest_edit` (`find`/`replace` → `deletion`+`insertion` marks, `user` = "<name or email> (agent)"; picked up by `captureSuggestions` into the review queue), `prism_sheet_read`/`prism_sheet_update` (A1; live → per-cell Y.Array writes with the client's delete+insert idiom, else CSV RMW through the gateway PATCH with `if_updated_at`). These bypass `ctx.dispatch` for the Y.Doc, so they check permissions with `collabAccess` — a line-for-line mirror of `resolveLevel` (parity test) — AFTER the gateway GET view gate. Rules (unchanged by suggest-only enforcement — the MCP tools write through a server-side direct connection, not a client socket; only the raw socket's write threshold moved to edit): view to read; **≥ suggest to comment/resolve/suggest (comment-level actors may NOT comment via MCP — same as the editor, WP0.2)**; edit for content/cells. Yjs writes go through `hocuspocus.openDirectConnection(docName)` with origin `mcp:<email>`; `disconnect()` stores immediately (normal `storeDocumentState`) and unloads the doc if nobody else has it. Reads (`list_comments`, `sheet_read`) never open a connection — live doc if loaded, else the SQLite snapshot / vault CSV. **`prism_update_note` on a LIVE doc** = three-way merge: vault `updatedAt` must equal `if_updated_at` (else stale `conflict`), and the snapshot row's TRUE BASE (`getDocState().base`: the snapshot itself when in step, the kept `base_state` while it is ahead of the vault, null = unknown) must be of that same version and an ancestor of the live state (else `conflict {live, retry}`); the agent's content is applied to a FORK of that base (documents/code via `applyExternalContent`, sheets cell-by-cell, canvas changed-elements-only with `version` bumped) and only the fork's delta is applied to the live doc — unsaved human edits survive. Not-live notes stay on the REST path (routing every write through Yjs would HTML-ify Markdown notes). A metadata/tag/path-only write to a live doc calls `markReconciled(docName, prevMs, nextMs)` so the reconciler does not fold the content-stale vault copy back over unsaved typing (it advances only if `prevMs` was already absorbed). Adding a comment/suggestion to a never-opened Markdown note converts its stored body to collab HTML (same as a first live open). Tests: `test/mcp-collab.test.ts` (real Hocuspocus + WS provider "humans"; sets `hocuspocus.configuration.debounce` high so human edits stay unsaved).
- **Governance / sharing / dashboard tools (WP6.4):** `mcp/tool-governance.ts` (`prism_governance_state`, `prism_propose_change`, `prism_vote`, `prism_withdraw_proposal`; resources `prism://governance/constitution` + `prism://me`; prompts `review-open-proposals`, `summarize-comments`, `edit-shared-doc`), `mcp/tool-sharing.ts` (`prism_note_access`, `prism_share`), `mcp/tool-dashboard.ts` (`prism_dashboard_query`, runs `@prism/core/dashboard-engine` = the pure `filter-engine.ts` over the actor's viewable notes). All go through `/api/governance/*` dispatch; **prompts** are a new `PrismPrompt` registry (`buildMcpServer` 5th arg, `PRISM_PROMPTS`). **`/acl` decision:** `dispatchAsActor` stays `/api`-only; sharing gets `ctx.dispatchShare` (`dispatchShareAsActor` + `safeSharePath`) with an EXACT allowlist of the 5 scoped-share routes (chosen over /api wrappers: same handler + `denyEscalation`, no duplicated enforcement path, non-share /acl unreachable by construction — tested). MCP is stricter than the web: `prism_share` refuses an email with no password account for EVERYONE (no invites via MCP), and never returns link/invite URLs. `prism_vote` refuses `amend_governance` proposals (constitution edits are web-only) and auto-tries `/apply` only for content proposals (never publishes staged revisions); `prism_withdraw_proposal` is proposer-only. `ContentPayload` gained `rationale` (voter-visible, never written to the note). Early `access()` for governance = standing (member/grant/active governance membership, 15 s cached). Tests: `test/mcp-governance.test.ts` (integrity ON).
- **Gotchas:** `WWW-Authenticate` values must be ASCII (a non-Latin-1 char in an error description 500s the response). `/mcp` + `/.well-known/` are in the SW `navigateFallbackDenylist`. Config: `MCP_RATE_PER_MINUTE` (120/credential), `MCP_AUTH_FAILURES_PER_10MIN` (20/IP), `MCP_OWNER_HUB_SUBS`. `authorization_servers` is deliberately absent from the metadata until the hub can mint Prism-audience tokens (OAuth / claude.ai connectors = later).

**Connect your agent + legacy-token migration (WP6.5).** UI: `AgentAccessTokens.tsx` (Settings → Account → "Connect your agent") adds a vault picker (`AccountClient.listAgentVaults` → `GET /api/vaults`, so only vaults the user can access), copy blocks for Claude Code / Claude Desktop (mcp-remote bridge) / generic Streamable HTTP, and "Test connection" (`AccountClient.testAgentConnection`: stateless `tools/list` on `/mcp` via `serverFetch` with the new PAT, `credentials:"omit"`). The secret lives in component state only, dropped on Done/unmount. Server: owner-only (`config.ownerEmail`) `GET /api/mcp/legacy-tokens` + `POST /api/mcp/legacy-tokens/revoke {jtis?, notify?, dryRun?}` in `routes/mcp.ts` — **`dryRun` defaults TRUE**; revokes via the injected `revokeVaultToken`; a failed hub revoke stays active and un-notified; `notify` sends ONE `sendEmail` per member (injectable `setLegacyTokenNotifier`); audit table `mcp_token_revocations`. Never run automatically — the overseer runs the dry-run → owner approval → revoke-all+notify runbook in `docs/mcp-access.md`. UI card: Network → Server "Legacy agent tokens" (`CollabSharing.getLegacyMcpTokens/revokeLegacyMcpTokens`, web only). Tests: `apps/server/test/mcp-migration.test.ts`.

## Version history (Parachute vault ≥ 0.7.9)

The vault captures the prior content + metadata of a note on every update, append, delete and restore (20-version floor, 100 ceiling, 180 days, 8 MiB/note; per-vault `history:` block in `vault.yaml`, read at vault start). Prism surfaces it everywhere through the `VaultClient` seam — `listNoteVersions` / `getNoteVersion` / `restoreNoteVersion` (optional methods; `HistoryUnavailableError` on a 0.6.x vault, whose `/versions` 404s even for an existing note) — implemented by `HttpVaultClient` (gateway) and `TauriVaultClient` (Rust `vault_list_note_versions` / `vault_get_note_version` / `vault_restore_note_version`).

- **UI:** `components/layout/HistoryPanel.tsx` (context-panel History tab; also the "Version History" command and the mobile More sheet) — current version + day-grouped timeline, size deltas, paging. `components/history/VersionViewer.tsx` (portaled to `<body>`) — diff vs the current note ("what restoring does") or vs the version before ("what that save changed"), full text, ←/→ stepping, two-step Restore. Diffs come from `lib/history/diff.ts` (HTML → paragraphs, Excalidraw scene → element list, word-level edits, folded context).
- **Semantics:** a version row is the state *before* a change; `supersededAt`/`op` describe the change that replaced it, so a row's "saved at" is the NEXT-OLDER row's `supersededAt`. Restore brings back content + metadata only (tags and path stay), needs the reviewed `if_updated_at` (the vault refuses `force`), and captures the replaced state — every restore is undoable.
- **Restore safety (`useRestoreVersion`):** flush pending autosaves first (`flushPendingSaves` — the user's typing becomes a version, never lost) → restore against the post-flush `updatedAt` → `discardPendingSaves` → `bumpNoteRevision` so `Canvas` remounts plain renderers on the new content. Live collab docs keep their session: the server reconciler folds the restore in within ~2 s.
- **Collab gotcha:** `storeDocumentState` skips the vault write when the serialized doc equals the note's content — otherwise every fold/idle store would add a no-change history version.
- **Compaction:** the vault only compacts at open or via admin `POST /api/history/compact`; `worker/history-compact.ts` runs it daily per vault in short slices with an ephemeral admin token (`HISTORY_COMPACT_INTERVAL_MS`, 0 = off).
- **2 MB ceiling:** a note over 2,000,000 bytes cannot be updated while history is on — hence Matrix thread rollover. Upgrade plan + research: `docs/roadmap/parachute-upgrade/`.

## Dashboard Widget System

Dashboard notes (`tag: dashboard`) store their layout in `metadata.layout.widgets` — an array of `DashboardWidgetConfig` objects. The filter engine (`src/lib/dashboard/filter-engine.ts`) runs entirely client-side on notes fetched from the vault.

9 widget types are registered in `src/lib/dashboard/widget-registry.ts`: `list`, `board`, `gallery`, `stat`, `progress`, `timeline`, `chart`, `embed`, `quick-actions`. Each widget's `source` field specifies tags/pathPrefix/metadataFilters that determine which notes feed it.

## Typed properties + database views

**Model.** Values stay in `note.metadata[key]`; TYPES come from the vault tag schema (`type`/`enum`/`default`); Prism-only presentation hints (`kind`, `label`, option `colors`, `hidden`) live in server SQLite settings `schema-ui:<vaultId>:<tag>` and are merged into `GET /api/schemas`. A **database** is an ordinary note with `metadata.prism_type: "database"` + `metadata.prism_database = {version:1, source:{tags}, views:[{id,name,type: table|board|gallery|list|calendar, filter?, sort?, groupBy?, visible?, widths?, dateKey?, coverKey?, order?}]}` — rows ARE the tagged notes (never copied); unknown configs fail closed (`components/database/config.ts`). Pure engine shared by server + client fallback: `@prism/core/database` (`lib/database/{query,schema}.ts`).

**Server (`apps/server/src/routes/databases.ts`, mounted in `api.ts` BEFORE the owner short-circuit).** `GET /api/schemas[?tags=]` → `{schemas, canEdit}` (non-owners see a tag only if they can VIEW a note carrying it — from the tree projection — or hold a grant naming it; no counts; `canEdit` = owner role); `PUT /api/schemas/:tag` (OWNER role + `csrfRefusal`; additive only: new field needs a type, no type change, enum append-only, no enum on free text or arrays; `agent-skill`/`agent-dispatch`/`governance-*` refused 403; ingest tags (`email`, `meeting`, `message-thread`, `person`, `transcript`, `task`, …) refuse `default:` (409 `protected_tag`); a NEW field whose name existing notes use with another value type → 409 `type_conflict`; one read-merge-write at a time per (vault, tag); vault write via a minted ephemeral admin token, injectable `setSchemaAdminMinter`); `POST /api/query {tags[1-5], filter{match all|any, conditions≤25}, sort≤3, limit≤500, cursor, fields?, search?, tzOffset?}` → `{rows(+canEdit), next, total, limited, truncated}` (CSRF + per-actor rate `QUERY_RATE_PER_MINUTE` 120, owner 600; a non-admin asking for a tag they cannot see gets an empty page with NO vault call; ONE canonical lean listing per (vault, tag) — schema keys + row/permission keys, whole metadata for a schema-less tag — ordered `updated_at` desc, cached 4 s, LRU-capped `QUERY_CACHE_MAX_ENTRIES` 24 / `QUERY_CACHE_MAX_ROWS` 150k; `fields` only projects; the `QUERY_SCAN_MAX` (20k) cut happens AFTER permission filtering, so `truncated` never counts hidden notes; `limited` = "permission-filtered", never "something was hidden"; `@today` and date-vs-datetime comparisons use the caller's `tzOffset`); `POST /api/properties/:id {set, expect?}` (CSRF; strict note-id shape, an alias resolving to another id → 404; metadata-only, `edit` cap, `prism_*`/`gov_*` refused, per-field CAS → 409 `{fields, current}`, vault `if_updated_at` retried once, `markReconciled` when the collab doc is live). Tests: `test/databases.test.ts`, `test/database-engine.test.ts`.

**Client.** Optional seam `VaultClient.getSchemas/updateSchema/queryNotes/updateProperties` (web `rest.ts`); shells without them (or an older server answering 404/405/501 — never a 403) fall back to bundled `tag-schemas.json`, the same engine over a bounded `listNotes`, and `updateNote` with client-side CAS (`lib/database/hooks.ts`; `noteAccess()` derives edit affordances from `_caps`/`_level`/row `canEdit`). UI in `components/database/`: `PropertyBar` (under the title in `DocumentRenderer`, via `NotePropertyBar` in `CollabDoc`, schema fields in `MetadataPanel`; never on `readOnly`/published surfaces), `DatabaseRenderer` + `views.tsx` + `ViewControls.tsx`, `OpenAsDatabaseButton` (tag page). Fixture e2e: `e2e-fixtures/databases.spec.ts`, `page-properties.spec.ts`.

**Database depth (wave 2C).** Engine (`lib/database/query.ts`): `filter.groups` = ONE level of `{match, conditions}` groups, each one term of the top-level match (≤5 groups, ≤25 conditions in all, a nested `groups` is a 400); `search` matches the title OR any string/string[] value of `fields` (else every non-system key; `prism_*`/`gov_*`/`_*` never searched). **Writer stamp** `metadata.prism_last_writer` + `prism_last_write_at` (`apps/server/src/writer-stamp.ts`): the value is an OPAQUE subject id `u_<16 hex>` = HMAC(SESSION_SECRET, email) — never an email — or `"link"`; stamped by `POST /properties/:id`, `/properties/batch`, the CSV import, non-owner `POST /notes` + content/metadata `PATCH /notes/:id`, and the owner passthrough's single-note POST/PATCH JSON bodies that carry content or metadata (tag/path-only bodies and bodies with integers beyond 2^53 pass through byte-for-byte; a client value is overwritten). `resolveWriter()` turns it into a display name (users.name, else email) ONLY in `/api/query` for signed-in viewers (before the engine, so it sorts/filters by name), and answers null when the note was written again >10 s after the stamp (collab stores, ingest, desktop, agents) so "Last edited by" never contradicts "Last edited time". **Identity keys** (`identity-keys.ts`: `prism_creator`, `prism_last_writer`, `prism_last_write_at`, `prism_trashed_by`) are stripped for capability links (`/api/query` before the engine — no filter oracle — `/notes`, `/notes/:id`, versions), the public site (`/api/p/:slug/notes/:id`) and GitHub frontmatter; vault mirrors drop the writer stamp (they keep `prism_creator`/`prism_visibility`, which decide who sees a private copy). Rotating SESSION_SECRET orphans old stamps (they resolve to nothing). Engine helpers on user values are linear (`unwrapLink`, `looksLikeEmail`; never an unanchored `.*` regex — `test/database-review.test.ts` is time-bounded). `POST /api/properties/batch {items:[{id,set,expect?}] 1–100}` (registered BEFORE `/properties/:id`; 512 KB body; CSRF; per-actor item rate `PROPERTY_BATCH_ITEMS_PER_MINUTE` 600, admin 2000) → `{results:[{id, ok, updatedAt, metadata} | {id, ok:false, error: not_found|forbidden|locked|conflict(+fields,current)|vault_rejected|vault_error}]}`, 200 all ok / 207 partial; each item = the single route's rules (unviewable == missing). Trashed pages refuse property writes (404). `POST /api/databases/import/csv {tag, csv ≤2 MB, mapping {col: "$title"|key|""}, keyColumn?, pathPrefix, dryRun=true}` — owner/admin + CSRF, Prism-managed tags 403, ≤2,000 rows/60 cols, RFC 4180 linear parser (`lib/database/csv.ts`), values coerced by the vault schema (bad value/option = a ROW error), matched by the key column (default title) against a FRESH dedicated lean listing of exactly the mapped keys (trashed excluded; ambiguous/repeated keys = row errors), updates only differing cells with CAS (`if_updated_at` of the planned revision, never forced; a 409 is reported), creates at `<pathPrefix>/<title>`; re-running converges. Tests `test/database-depth.test.ts`.
**Client (wave 2C).** `prism_database` gains `openIn` (side|center|page), `templates [{id,name}]` + `defaultTemplate` (template = a note under `<db>/Templates/` WITHOUT the source tags, body + `prism_template_props`), view filters may carry `groups`. UI: `RowPeek` (side/center peek renders the row through the same renderer/collab seam as a tab; Esc closes; ⌘-click/phones/`page` open the tab), `Templates.tsx` (+ New ▾, template editor), `BulkBar.tsx` (table selection: checkbox, shift-range, ⌘A; bulk edit = `useBatchPropertyWriter` with per-row expect + one CAS Undo; duplicate; Trash via `trashPage` + Undo via `restoreFromTrash`; only for people who can act), `Csv.tsx` (export any view, formula-guarded; import dialog → dry run → write), collapsible table/list groups, `SYSTEM_PROPERTIES` (created/edited time = note columns, created/edited by = `prism_creator`/`prism_last_writer`, read-only, sort/filter), `email`/`phone` kinds, relation hints `relationTag` (picker target) + `reverseLabel` (read-only reverse list on target pages via `useReverseRelations`, an `eq` query on the forward key — never written back). **Inline blocks:** `DatabaseBlock`/`renderDatabaseBlock(noteId, viewId?)` (+ `databaseBlockHtml`, `parseDatabaseBlock`, `createInlineDatabase`, `addLinkedView`) — the editor's `databaseView` atom (owned by the editor schema group) stores `<div data-prism-database="<id>" data-view="<viewId>">`. Seam: optional `VaultClient.updatePropertiesBatch/importCsv`. Task boards: per-column "+ Add task", card ⋯ menu, due chips, scroller fades/buttons, "Open as database" (new database note, tasks untouched). Fixtures: `notion-db-views|props|inline|csv.spec.ts`, `databases.spec.ts` (AND/OR), `boards.spec.ts`.

**Wave 4C (databases; NP-DB-01/03/04/05/07/08/11/12/16/23/25).**
- **Property management is presentation-only (🔒 NP-DB-11).** The vault tag schema is shared by every note with the tag and stays additive-only (`PUT /api/schemas/:tag` still refuses a type change / option removal). Everything else is a schema-ui hint (`FieldHints` in `lib/database/schema.ts`, validated by `validateSchemaPatch`, merged into `GET /api/schemas`): rename = `label` (the metadata key never moves); change type = `kind`, only between presentations of the field's VAULT type (`compatibleKinds`: string → text/url/email/phone/date/select/status/person/relation, array → multi_select/person/relation/files, number, boolean, date, reference; anything else → 409 `incompatible_kind`, checked on the merged schema so create-with-kind works); options = `optionLabels` (stored value → name; two options can't share a name), `colors`, `optionOrder`, `hiddenOptions` (an option "delete": 409 `option_in_use {count}` while a non-trashed page holds it; the vault enum keeps it); `format` (number: number|comma|percent|usd|eur|gbp); `statusGroups` (option → todo|in_progress|complete; default guessed by `statusGroupOf`); `deleted` = hidden on EVERY surface (`resolveProperties` skips it and claims the key, `deletedKeys` keeps it out of the free-key scan; restorable from View settings / Add property → "Deleted properties"). Stored values are never rewritten by any of these; option chips show `PropertyOption.label`, CSV export writes stored values.
- **The only data deletion:** `POST /api/schemas/:tag/fields/:field/remove-values {dryRun=true, limit≤2000 (500)}` — owner role, CSRF, human origin only (`requestVia` session/device; MCP/local-token → 403 `agent_origin_refused`); `LOCKED_TAG` 403, `INGEST_TAGS` 409 `protected_tag`, system/ingest keys 400; a WRITE needs the field already `deleted` (409 `not_deleted`; a dry run may preview first). Skips trashed pages, system notes, and pages carrying ANOTHER tag whose vault schema declares the same key; one `if_updated_at` write per page (`{[field]: null}`, never forced; 409 → `conflicts`), concurrency 2, one run per (vault, tag, field) (409 `busy`), `markReconciled` for live docs, `action_audit` row `schema.remove-values` with counts only. Response `{dryRun, tag, field, total, skipped{trashed,shared,system}, truncated, removed?, conflicts?, failed?, remaining?}`. Seam: optional `VaultClient.removePropertyValues`. UI: `components/database/PropertyEditor.tsx` (table header menu "Edit property…", the property label on a page; owners only — `canEdit` from `GET /api/schemas`). Tests `test/database-props.test.ts`.
- **Review round (🔒).** *remove-values* also skips and counts `skipped.ingest` (a page with `protectionReason`, ANY tag in `INGEST_TAGS`, or an ingest `metadata.source` — an `[email, proj]` note keeps `uid`/`lastMessageAt`) and `skipped.private` (someone else's private page); "shared" also consults the other tags' schema-ui HINTS; every lookup is `Object.hasOwn`; `isPrototypeName` (any `Object.prototype` property) is refused as a field, hint or option name in both validators. A write run is one of ≤ `limit` pages / ~20 s (`SCHEMA_REMOVE_BUDGET_MS`) and answers `more` — the client loops (each run re-lists); ONE run server-wide (409 `busy`); per-owner limits `SCHEMA_REMOVE_PER_MINUTE` (30) and `SCHEMA_WRITES_PER_MINUTE` (120, PUT + availability). *Hints:* an option label may not read as another option's value or label (409 `option_name_taken`); a stored `kind` is re-checked when the field is declared later; no hints for system keys or for `prism-trashed` / `SYSTEM_NOTE_TAGS` / `governance-*` tags (403); labels pass `cleanLabel` (no line breaks, no bidi controls). *Deleted keys:* a property deleted on tag A never hides tag B's live same-key property (`deletedKeys` = deleted by one tag and live in none). *New database from CSV:* the SERVER decides the tag is new — `PUT /api/schemas/:tag {requireNew:true}` and `GET /api/schemas/:tag/availability` (owner) refuse a tag with pages or properties (`tag_in_use`), one a grant names or a site publishes (`tag_governed`), ingest tags, and Prism-managed tags (403); the client creates the PAGE first (unconfigured), then the schema, then the view config against the page's fresh revision, then the rows; a refusal trashes the page it just made; `NewDatabaseError.progress` makes a retry continue. *Integrations:* `INGEST_TAGS`, `INGEST_SOURCE_VALUES` (pinned equal to the server's `INGEST_SOURCES`) and `integrationOwned(row)` live in `lib/database/schema.ts`; the calendar never drags an integration-owned row (title + context-menu say why; `/api/query` rows carry `source`, `source_id`, `calendarEventId`); date RANGES are not offered on fields of ingest tags (`task.due`); `dueSummary`/`dueSortKey` (`dates.ts`) make the task-board due chip and Home's task order read a range as start – end.
- **Dates (`lib/database/dates.ts`, pure, linear):** a date value is `YYYY-MM-DD`, a datetime (the editor writes a zoned instant) or a range `start/end`. Engine (`query.ts`): a range sorts by its start, `eq` matches any day inside it, `gt/gte` read its END and `lt/lte` its START (so a `>= from AND <= to` window keeps overlapping ranges). The inline `<input type=date>` still edits a plain day; "Time and end date" opens `DatePicker` (ranges only when the vault type is not `date`). Tests `test/database-dates.test.ts`.
- **Views.** `DatabaseView` gains `hideEmptyGroups` and `cardSize` (small|medium|large); `duplicateView`/`moveView` (`config.ts`; tabs also reorder by HTML5 drag for people who can save). Table: arrow keys move between body cells, Enter edits, Esc returns to the cell, Tab commits and moves on (`onGridKey` in `views.tsx`; `PropertyValue` restores focus). Tab / Shift+Tab inside the table are handled by the table (`walkTab`: next / previous stop in document order; the browser's key at the first / last stop, never a trap) — Safari's default Tab skips buttons, so from a cell editor it used to commit and leave the table. `walkTab` is per widget; nothing handles Tab globally (other widgets still follow the browser's Tab rule). Calendar: week rows; a range is ONE bar per week row (`weekBars`); drag (dnd-kit, per-chip `useDraggable`, day `useDroppable`) moves a value by whole days (`shiftDateValue` keeps time of day + range length) through the per-field CAS writer; a conflict is refused, said, and the rows refresh. Phones render a board as its grouped list first ("Show as board" toggles; the saved view is untouched).
- **Relations (NP-DB-12).** Chips open the linked page (`useOpenLinked`: plain click where read-only, the picker's chip, or ⌘/Ctrl-click in an editable cell). The reverse property on the target page is editable (`ReverseEditor` in `PropertyBar.tsx`): it writes the LINKING page's forward value with per-field CAS and nothing on the target, so there is still one stored side.
- **Creating.** New page menu → "Database" (asks for its source tag; `CONTENT_DEFAULTS.database`); slash "Full-page database" (`DatabaseInsertRequest.mode: "page"`: sub-page + a `[[path]]` link, opens the tab). **CSV → new database:** `CsvNewDatabaseDialog` / `importCsvAsNewDatabase` / `planNewDatabase` (exported from `@prism/core` for the import entry): the tag must be unused; schema writes in ≤20-field batches, then the database page (or `adopt` an unconfigured one — "Or import a CSV as its rows…"), then the existing import route.

## Shell, sync state, search and offline polish (wave 2E)

- **One truthful sync state** (`packages/core/src/lib/sync/syncState.ts`, `SyncStateBadge`). `Shell` re-provides the VaultClient wrapped by `trackVaultWrites` (memoised Proxy; only write methods are wrapped): a write counts as in flight until it settles AND `hasPendingWrites()` has been re-read, so an outbox-queued write never reads "Saved". Other inputs: `useAutoSave` (`markDirty` while debounced, `reportSaveFailure` with its own `saveNow` retry — **a 409/428 is never retryable: the draft goes to the outbox as a `conflict` row via `preserveDraft(…, "conflict")` → "Needs review"**), the web `OfflineIndicator` (`reportPendingWrites(count, needsReview)`; listens for `prism:open-saved-changes`; toasts `prism:offline-refused`), live collab docs (`reportSyncSource`; socket down with unsynced edits = `local`, never "Saved"). `deriveSyncStatus` order: failed → review → offline/local → saving → waiting → saved. Variants: `header` (TabBar desktop), `phone` (dot + "Saved on this device"), `footer` ("Synced", mounted in Navigation). **`App`'s QueryClient uses `networkMode: "offlineFirst"` (queries) / `"always"` (mutations)** — the TanStack default paused both offline, so cached pages never reached the read cache and offline saves sat in memory instead of the durable outbox. Don't revert it.
- **Offline writes (`apps/web/src/offline/outbox.ts` + `rest.ts` `writeJson`) — what queues, what retries, what refuses.**
  - **Queued offline and retried automatically** (backoff 1.5 s → 60 s) when the server gives no answer / 5xx / 429 — rows for which a duplicate delivery is harmless (`retrySafe`): content saves WITH a base revision (coalesced per note into one row: first base, latest content), metadata merges without a base (`kind: "meta"`: properties, icon, font — coalesced; each key keeps its PRE-EDIT value in `expect` and replays per key through `POST /api/properties/:id {set, expect}`, or read + CAS PATCH for `prism_*`/older servers; a key changed elsewhere → review naming the keys), tag/link deltas, and creates (every create carries `metadata.prism_client_op`; after an unconfirmed outcome or a 409 the outbox looks the path up and ADOPTS its own page, else review with "Retry" / "Create under another name"). A replay 409 whose content/values are already on the server is success (lost acknowledgement).
  - **Kept for review, never re-sent blindly:** a content save with no base revision; any row that hit a real conflict (409), a missing page (404/410) or lost access (401/403).
  - **Refused — an error + toast, nothing queued, never an optimistic success — offline AND when the server is unreachable/5xx:** rename/move (`path`; with unsent rows for the page it flushes first, else refuses), delete, nested metadata values (`prism_database`, dashboard `layout`, board config: one object to the vault), every pages route (move/trash/restore/lock/order/style), version restore, everything else.
  - **Invariants (do not break):** `flush()` re-reads each row, `claim()`s it, resolves references FROM THE CLAIMED ROW and sends only that body; `confirm()` deletes a row only if it still holds what was sent (an edit folded in during a flush pass used to be dropped under a "Saved" badge). A `sending` row is never folded into. Every confirmed delivery re-bases later queued saves made on the revision it replaced, records it in the per-tab `superseded` map (shared over `BroadcastChannel("prism-outbox")`, read by `currentBase()`), and fires `prism:note-confirmed` (Shell re-reads the page). A stuck or waiting row holds back only LATER rows of the SAME note. `localNote` overlays only rows that will be sent (`queued`/`sending`) and MERGES metadata. Discarding a stuck row sends later rows of that note to review. Queued creates are part of `listTree` (unique paths). A dead sender's `sending` row returns to the queue after 35 s if retry-safe. Rows never replay under another account/vault (`sameScope`); rows of other scopes are counted in the dialog and purged after 30 days; `logout()` asks Stay / Download / Discard when unsent rows exist; an IndexedDB write failure raises `prism:storage-failed` (standing banner). Rows are plaintext in IndexedDB.
  - Specs: `notion-outbox.spec.ts`, `notion-offline-writes.spec.ts`, `outbox.spec.ts` (fixture enforces CAS; `window.prismShell.failures/holdIds/unreachable`).
- **Live documents offline (`apps/web/src/collab/unsynced.ts`):** a per-scope registry of documents whose local Yjs state the server has not taken (marked while the socket is down and on leaving with unacknowledged changes; cleared ONLY by a completed sync). Not-open entries make the badge read "Changes on this device — will sync"; on reconnect each is synced through a headless provider after a fresh authorization (edit level only — read-only scope, `update_required` or lost access keep the entry and the local state). `CollabDoc` opens without a connection only when the document already has local state, using the last authorized cached read. Spec: `notion-collab-offline.spec.ts`.
- **Header (NP-PG-06):** save state, star, labelled Share, ⋯, labelled Agent (dot from the CACHED agent session list — `useAgentActive` never fetches) in one row; `TabBar` must work without a QueryClientProvider (fixtures mount it bare).
- **Search (`GET /api/search`, `routes/search.ts`, mounted in `api.ts` before the owner short-circuit).** The full-text route for every actor on the gateway (ranked search stays `/api/search/semantic` in `routes/rag.ts`; the owner's vault passthrough `GET /api/notes?search=` still exists and is what MCP uses for admins). `q` ≤200, `limit` 1–100, `title=1`, `type=` (inferContentType), `tag=`, `author=<email>|me` (creator), `editor=me` (last editor — see *Wave 8 gaps*), `after`/`before`, `date=created`, `lean=1`; rows = `Note` + `_matches {title, snippet, snippetMatches}` (+ `_caps` for signed-in non-owners). View filter + trash exclusion first, filters only narrow, no totals; ONE vault call ≤100 rows, identical in-flight queries coalesced (`sharedVaultSearch`; rows are permission-filtered per caller afterwards); a snippet reads ≤20 KB per note (`contentWindow`); `SEARCH_RATE_PER_MINUTE` (owner 600 / others 120). Pure helpers `@prism/core/search` (`lib/search/match.ts`): **`plainText` is a single linear pass with NO regex over note content** (an earlier wikilink regex was quadratic on unmatched `[[` — 44 s at 100k chars; `test/search.test.ts` has time-bounded pathological inputs — keep it that way, same rule as `proton-parse.ts`); numeric entities are range-checked. Client: `VaultClient.searchNotes?` (null on an older server → `search` + client filters), `useVaultSearch(query, filters?)`, `navigation/searchFilters.tsx` (title only, type, edited by me, date, **vault scope** = `X-Prism-Vault` on the request, server-side only) / `searchHighlight.tsx` / `searchRecents.ts`. Ranked search is used only without filters.
- **Offline pages (NP-OF-04, `apps/web/src/offline/availableOffline.ts` + `readCache.ts`):** favorites + last 20 opened + pinned (page ⋯ "Make available offline", core seam `lib/offline/availability.ts`) are prefetched through `rest.getNote` (gateway checks). Quiet: only while visible, hourly, changed-only by the tree's `updatedAt` (`rest.treeStamps`), ≤60/pass, 2 at a time. Privacy: cached bodies expire after 30 days (`MAX_AGE_MS`); every FRESH tree evicts cached pages it no longer lists plus cached lists (`reconcileCachedNotes`); "Remove offline copy" deletes the body; pinned entries have their own 200 MB budget (never evict tree/lists; the pinned set is persisted so the LRU never runs without it after a reload); share-link viewers' copies expire after 24 h and are all evicted when the link answers 401/403/404/410; sign-out, account change and a signed-out `/auth/me` (PWA 401) clear the cache AND `prism:offline-*` / `prism:recent-searches:*` in localStorage.
- **Per-page style (NP-PG-08):** `metadata.prism_page_style {small, full}` only via `POST /api/notes/:id/meta` (partial sets merge onto the stored style, both keys written as explicit booleans — the vault MERGES nested metadata; `edit` for style AND `organize` for lock/order when combined; locked → 423; the non-owner gateway PATCH refuses the key). `Canvas` sets `data-page-small/full` on `<main id="workspace-document">`; CSS in `styles/shell.css`. Font = the existing per-document `contentFont` (menu reuses `docFontSetter`).
- **New page in one action (NP-SB-13/MB-02, `lib/pages/quickCreate.ts`):** `usePagesUI.openCreate({})`/`{folder}` and the sidebar/phone "New page" create "Untitled" at once and click the title into edit mode; `{template}`/`{chooser}` (and a failed create) open `NewContentMenu` ("Choose page type").
- **Page chrome:** `BacklinksPill` (counts only sources in the permission-filtered tree; links fetched without the body), `EmptyPageStarters` (fills only this page; never after typing; live docs only after sync), phone `KeyboardToolbar` (visualViewport; caret kept clear via `--keyboard-toolbar-space`).
- **Shell:** collapsed-sidebar peek on left-edge hover or its keyboard trigger (`useSidebarPeek`; pinned sidebar and peek are mutually exclusive → one `<Navigation/>`), phone left-edge swipe = back else Browse (`useEdgeSwipe`: starts ≤20 px from the edge, never inside a sideways scroller), phone bottom bar = Notes · Inbox (Messages where the server has no inbox) · Search · Agent · More, motion tokens `--motion-fast/base/slow` (120–180 ms) zeroed by `prefers-reduced-motion` and the device-local **Settings → Appearance → Reduce motion** (`lib/motion.ts`, `html.reduce-motion`); generic `[role=menu]` only fades (`.prism-menu-enter` opts into the translate).
- **Form controls (WebKit pass fixes).** ONE rule styles every drop-down `<select>` (`styles/workspace.css`, `select:not([multiple]):not([size])`): `appearance: none` (desktop Safari otherwise draws a 20–23 px macOS pop-up button whatever CSS height it has), our chevron (two gradient halves in the select's own `currentColor`; mirrored for `:dir(rtl)` / `[dir=rtl]`) and `padding-inline-end: 26px` — those are `!important` because surfaces set `background:` / `padding:` shorthands and inline styles; height, border, background colour stay the surface's (a surface without a height gets none). Still a native select; `forced-colors` returns it to the system control. Do not add a second chevron (icon or background) to a select. **Settings section column:** `overflow-y: scroll` + a 205 px grid column — its layout must not depend on whether the scrollbar is there (WebKit does not re-lay the items when an `auto` scrollbar appears; `scrollbar-gutter` has no effect beside the custom `::-webkit-scrollbar`).
- Fixture: `e2e-fixtures/notion-shell.html` (real App + HttpVaultClient over an intercepted fetch that enforces the vault's CAS; `window.prismShell` holds/fails writes, edits "on the server", hides pages, switches account). Specs: `notion-{sync-state,page,search,offline,offline-writes,sidebar,mobile,a11y}.spec.ts`; server: `test/search.test.ts`, `test/page-style.test.ts`.

## Import, export, templates, print (wave 3A)

- **Pure half** = `@prism/core/import-export` (`packages/core/src/lib/import-export/`): `zip.ts` (dependency-free ZIP reader + streaming writer; the reader is for HOSTILE input — central directory only, every number bounds-checked, ZIP64/multi-disk/encrypted/other methods refused, entry-count + per-entry + total DECLARED-size limits before anything inflates, overlapping entries refused, an entry must inflate to exactly its declared size (`inflate(data, maxOut)` must throw past `maxOut`) and pass its CRC; `safeZipName` refuses absolute/drive/NUL/`.`/`..` names — such an entry is flagged `unsafe`, never read), `markdown.ts` (**linear scanners, no regex with an unbounded quantifier over content** — `rewriteMarkdownLinks`, `parseFrontMatter`/`renderFrontMatter` (values are JSON, so a value can never break out of its line), `attachmentIdsIn`, `stripNotionId`, `safeFileSegment`/`safePageSegment`; `test/transfer-pure.test.ts` has time-bounded pathological inputs — keep it that way), `plan.ts` (`planImport`: the import planner), `wire.ts`.
- **Export** (`routes/export.ts` + `transfer/export.ts`, mounted in `api.ts` before the owner short-circuit): `POST /api/export {scope:"page"|"vault", noteId?, format:"markdown"|"html", subpages?=true, attachments?=true}` → 202 `{jobId,total}` · `GET /api/export/:id` (progress) · `GET /api/export/:id/download` (the ZIP, streamed from a 0600 temp file in a 0700 dir, `EXPORT_DIR`) · `DELETE /api/export/:id`. A signed-in PERSON only (session/device; links/anon 401, MCP + loopback token 403); `vault` scope needs admin, `page` scope VIEW on the page (strict id; unviewable = missing = trashed = 404); POST `csrfRefusal`, DELETE origin checks; one running export per account, `EXPORT_MAX_CONCURRENT` 2, `EXPORT_STARTS_PER_HOUR` 20; jobs are in memory, bound to (account) + a 128-bit id, kept `EXPORT_TTL_MS` (15 min). **What goes in is the CALLER's view, for every role**: candidates = tree projection filtered by `effectiveCaps(...).has("view")` (so an owner's export does NOT contain another member's private notes), trashed excluded; each note is re-read by id and re-checked on the fresh note; role + grants re-read every 200 notes; an attachment only when its index row is in this vault and its OWNING note is viewable (else the link is left as it was); front matter drops identity keys and every `prism_*`/`gov_*`/`_*` key; `skipped` never counts a note the caller could not see. Markdown = front matter + `# Title` + body (HTML bodies through turndown); HTML = a standalone file with a `default-src 'none'` CSP meta (stored script cannot run from `file://`); spreadsheets/canvas/code export raw. Bounds: `EXPORT_MAX_NOTES` 50k, `EXPORT_MAX_BYTES` 2 GB, classic-ZIP limits (no ZIP64), per-note reads two at a time (never a content listing). Not done: a database is exported as its page (rows are ordinary pages with front matter), not as CSV.
- **Import** (`routes/import.ts` + `transfer/import.ts`): `POST /api/import?dryRun=1|0&parent=<folder>&name=<file>` with the FILE as the body (a .zip — incl. a Notion export and Notion's zip-of-parts wrapper, one level — or one .md/.html/.csv) → dry run (the DEFAULT) 200 `ImportPreview`, write 202 `{jobId, preview}`; `GET|DELETE /api/import/:id`. **Owner/admin only**, as a person. CSRF without JSON: REQUIRED `X-Prism-Import: 1`, a non-form Content-Type, Sec-Fetch-Site/Origin — all before the body is read; `IMPORT_MAX_BYTES` 100 MB (buffered once, one request at a time → 503 `busy`), `IMPORT_MAX_TOTAL_BYTES` 300 MB unpacked, `IMPORT_MAX_NOTES` 5000, one write job per server (409), per-hour limits, one `admin.import` `action_audit` row per write (counts only). Mapping: nesting kept (`Page <id>.md` + `Page <id>/`), Notion ids stripped, internal links → `[[destination path]]`, referenced images/files → attachments of the page through the SAME vault storage + `prism_attachments` index + sniff/quota rules as an editor upload, `Name.csv` (+ its folder) → a database note (`prism_database`, tag `db-<slug>-<hash>`) whose rows are the pages with the CSV columns as properties, front matter → tags + properties, HTML → Markdown (script/style/frames removed; **the server's DOM is a fragment parser — `htmlBody()` cuts the document down first**). Writes: root through `placementRefusal`; every path re-checked (`normalizePagePath` identity, `isProtectedPath`, `exportedLocation`, not under a trashed page); tags through `canonicalTag` and DROPPED when reserved (`sync-reserved.ts`), protected, published or named by any grant (an import never shares or publishes as a side effect); metadata keys never `prism_*`/`gov_*`/ingest/scheduler keys. **Idempotent**: every imported note carries `metadata.prism_import {v, src, hash, body, at}` — same `hash` → unchanged; changed source → updated (CAS) only if the stored body still equals `body`, else `conflict`; a path held by a note this import did not write is never overwritten (`if_exists: "error"`); a note whose attachments did not all land keeps no `hash` and is completed by the next run.
- **Templates (NP-TX-02)** `lib/pages/templates.ts`: `@today`, `@now`, `@me`/`@creator` resolve once, at creation (`applyTemplateVariables` in `NewContentMenu`; database row templates in `DatabaseRenderer.createRow`). HTML bodies get the editor's date `mention` chip, Markdown bodies text; a property whose value IS a token becomes `YYYY-MM-DD` / ISO / the creator's name. Not tokens: text inside tags, `<code>`/`<pre>`/fences/inline code, wikilinks, an existing chip, `a@today.io`. The creator's name comes from `/auth/me` via `serverFetch` (no server → `@me` stays).
- **Save as template + Templates gallery (NP-TX-01).** A page template is a note tagged `template` (`TEMPLATE_TAG`) — the ONE convention the New page chooser and the gallery both read; database row templates (`<db>/Templates/`) are a different thing and never listed. **🔒 A template never widens who can read the page it came from (review B1) — rules, do not relax:** (1) `templateSource` (`lib/pages/model.ts`) ALWAYS writes `prism_visibility: "private"`; the owner/admin path sends `prism_creator` = the saver (`CollabSharing.getViewer().email` — no viewer read → the menu item is hidden and the save refuses), the member route stamps the creator itself. Sharing is a deliberate act: the gallery's "Share" / "Make private" toggle (`prism_visibility: null` ⇄ `private` + creator), offered only to someone whose read carries no `_caps` (owner/admin — a member cannot widen visibility through the gateway). (2) A template carries NO source tag: `tags = ["template"]`; the page's tags are remembered in `metadata.prism_template_tags` (`TEMPLATE_TAGS_KEY`; canonical strings, never system/ingest tags — `templateTagsOf`) and re-applied by `templateCopy` on Use through the normal create rules; a 403 there → the page is created WITHOUT the tags and a toast names them (`NewContentMenu`), never a failed create. (3) Every template goes to `Templates/<free name>` — never beside the page (a sibling inherits the parent's page share); a 409 (a name held by a template the saver cannot see) retries once under a distinct name. (4) Server defence in depth: a `template`-tagged note is excluded from every publication (`canPublicView` + `pathPublicationIncludes` → listing, single note, graph, map, attachments), from `/api/query` and the shared engine (`runQuery`) unless the view's tags name `template`, from `useNotes` lists filtered by another tag and Home's task fallback (a template of a task is not a task), and its mention chips notify and link nobody (`noteContentStored`). (5) `duplicateCopy` keeps a private page's duplicate private (creator = the duplicator). **Copy bodies** (`lib/pages/copyBody.ts` `cleanCopyBody`, pure + LINEAR tag scanner, time-bounded test): Save, Use and Duplicate all drop pending suggested insertions, unwrap suggested deletions and comment anchors, drop `child-page` rows, and give every mention chip a NEW `data-mention-uid` without `data-reminder` — on stored HTML and on a live editor's `getHTML()` alike (`registeredEditor(noteId)`; a plain page is flushed and re-read). `templateKeepsKey` drops `prism_*` (except `prism_type`/`prism_page_style`/`prism_database`), `gov_*`, `_*`, `title`, `sync`, `forked_by`, every ingest key (`TEMPLATE_INGEST_KEYS`, pinned to the server's `INGEST_KEYS`) and ingest `source` values. Then `copyAttachments`. Gallery `components/pages/TemplatesGallery.tsx` (`usePagesUI.openTemplates`; chooser "Manage templates…", command "Templates", the save toast): Use (`openCreate({use})`), Edit, Rename (`metadata.title`, then a best-effort file move; ONE request per commit), Share/Make private, Delete (`trashPage`; Undo in the dialog and as the page toast after it closes). `PageMenuPopover` re-places itself when its item count changes. Tests: `test/template-keys.test.ts`, `test/template-server.test.ts`, `notion-templates.spec.ts`. Residual: templates made before this rule (tag `template` beside other tags, not private) keep working and keep their own visibility — only the server exclusions protect them. **Review round 3 — 🔒 `prism_template_tags` are TAGS.** The key is member-writable metadata that becomes tags on someone else's create, so: (server, `templateTagsCheck` in `routes/api.ts`) a non-owner create/PATCH value must be an array of ≤ 20 tag names (else 400), is stored canonical, may hold no system/trash/`template` tag, and each tag must be ungoverned or one the writer could add (`canAddTag`), else 403 `forbidden`; restating the stored value and `null` pass. A non-owner create of ANY note tagged `template` is forced `prism_visibility: private` (+ the stamped creator). **Member templates:** a signed-in workspace MEMBER (role ≥ member; never a guest or a link) may create a note whose ONLY tag is `template` exactly one segment below `Templates/` with no tag grant (`ownTemplate`; `placementRefusal` still applies) — so `canTemplate` = any non-guest with a viewer read. (client) `templateWantsTags(template)` = remembered tags + a hand-made template's own tags, never system or INGEST tags (`copyRefusesTag` includes `INGEST_TAGS`: a `[template, task]` template makes no `task`). They are applied silently ONLY for the viewer's own template (`_creator.me` / own `prism_creator`, judged on the FRESH read); for anyone else's the chooser lists them under "Tags from this template" as checkboxes, all unticked (the client cannot know which are published or shared). The 403 fallback matches the transport's `status`, not message text. **Reserved tag:** a note carrying a user tag named `template` is treated as a template everywhere — excluded from `/api/query` views and by-tag lists (`isTemplateNote`, ONE predicate: engine, `useNotes`, Home fallback, `TaskBoardRenderer`), from publications and from mention notifications/backlinks. **Copies:** only DOCUMENT bodies are cleaned (`copyBodyOf`: code / spreadsheet / canvas / website / presentation / dashboard / database notes are copied byte for byte); Duplicate therefore yields the "all suggestions rejected" state (suggested insertions gone, suggested deletions kept as text, comment anchors gone). "Share" in the gallery only clears private visibility — who then sees the template still follows its location and tags. Tests: `test/template-tags.test.ts`.
- **Print (NP-TX-06)** `styles/print.css`: `@media print` flattens every ancestor of `#workspace-document` (`:has()`) to an auto-height block and removes every sibling along that chain (sidebar, tabs, panels, dialogs, toasts), hides in-page controls, and re-declares the LIGHT palette (tokens.css + workspace.css `html.light` blocks — keep in step) so both themes print dark-on-white; transitions are off in print. Page ⋯ "Print", command "Print Page", Export → PDF all call `printCurrentPage()`; ⌘P is handled only in the native shell (browsers print natively).
- **Client**: `lib/import-export/{client,store}.ts` (`transferApi` over `serverFetch` + `setTransferContextHeaders` — the web shell passes the active-vault headers), `components/import-export/*` (`ExportDialog`, `ImportDialog`, `ImportExportHost` mounted in `Shell`). Entry points: page ⋯ "Export…" / "Print", command bar "Export Page…", "Print Page", and (owner/admin via `useCanManageTransfers`) "Import…" / "Export Workspace…". One page without sub-pages or files is still made on the device (`usePageActions.exportPage`). A PWA downloads archives over 150 MB by direct navigation; in the Prism Client the SHELL saves the archive (`save_export`, see *Native links and ⌘N*) — its webview cancels anchor downloads. Legacy desktop: no server → only the single-file export + print.
- **Review hardening (do not undo).**
  - *Nothing content-dependent runs on the event loop.* `transfer/worker-pool.ts` `TaskWorker` = ONE worker thread per pool (export conversion; import planning), one task at a time, a hard wall-clock limit per task (`worker.terminate()`; the clock starts when the task is handed to a READY worker). The worker entry is `worker-boot.mjs` (registers tsx inside the thread — a worker does not inherit loader hooks) → `worker.ts`. **The main thread never calls `marked` or turndown on note/upload content**: export pages go through `to-markdown` / `to-html` (`EXPORT_CONVERT_TIMEOUT_MS` 4 s, `EXPORT_CONVERT_MAX_BYTES` 1 MB, linear `htmlDepth` ≤ `EXPORT_CONVERT_MAX_DEPTH` 100); a page that cannot be converted is exported as plain text and listed in `_export.json` `plainText`. Import unzip + convert + hash + plan = `import-plan.ts` `planUpload` in the worker (`IMPORT_PLAN_TIMEOUT_MS` 45 s → 413 `too_complex`; HTML caps `IMPORT_MAX_HTML_FILE_BYTES` 512 KB / `_TOTAL_BYTES` 8 MB / `_DEPTH` 100 → that page is skipped); the upload buffer is TRANSFERRED to the worker and the referenced files come back transferred. The worker has no database: the tags an import may not apply are computed on the main thread and sent along (`deniedTags`). `worker.ts`/`import-plan.ts` must never import db/config/network. Tests assert the loop stays responsive (`test/transfer-review.test.ts`). The same worker (one implementation) serves collab and the Prism MCP through `convert/service.ts` — see *Content conversion — NEVER on the main thread* in the collab section.
  - *HTML export* body = `sanitizeHtml` (`lib/import-export/sanitize.ts`: a linear tokenizer that REBUILDS from an allowlist of elements/attributes, entity-decodes then checks URL schemes, drops script/style/iframe/svg/math/… with content) + CSP meta incl. `form-action 'none'; base-uri 'none'`. *Raw kinds* keep an extension only from `RAW_EXT`; `.html`/`.svg`/`.js`/`.bat`/… get `.txt` appended; spreadsheets leave formula-guarded (`csvCell`). Archive names `_export.json` / `_attachments/` / `_unfiled/` are reserved (a page with that name is written as `__…`).
  - *Export jobs:* timer sweep (60 s), shared on-disk budget `EXPORT_DISK_BUDGET_BYTES` (4 GB), and the download re-checks the account's CURRENT role / view of the exported page (lost → 404).
  - *Import:* `prism_import.assets {assetKey → attachmentId | "x"}` — a file is stored once per page across runs and updates; a permanently refused file ("x": wrong type, too large, active content) settles, so the page completes. The preview carries `audience {sharedPage, people, links, workspace}`; a write under a page share needs `confirmShared=1` (409 `confirm_shared`) or `private=1` (pages created `prism_visibility: private` + creator; the dialog defaults to private there). Imported text cannot become markup (turndown `escape` also escapes `<`), link targets other than http(s)/mailto/relative become their text, front-matter tags accept YAML flow lists. `resolveActions` verifies changed pages 4 at a time, at most `IMPORT_MAX_VERIFY` (1000; beyond → conflict). An unpacked wrapper part counts against `IMPORT_MAX_TOTAL_BYTES`; no upload is read while an import job runs (409). `prism_import` is in `INGEST_KEYS`: a non-admin can never write it.
  - `@me` in a template is the account's display name or the neutral "Me" — never the e-mail. A page created from a template calls `copyAttachments`.
  - The attachment store/sniff/quota steps are still replicated in `transfer/import.ts` (`attach`): `routes/attachments.ts` exports no shared upload function.
- Tests: `apps/server/test/{transfer-pure,export,import}.test.ts`; fixture `e2e-fixtures/notion-transfer.html` (= the pages-nav fixture + `transfer-server.ts`, an in-page fake of both routes that runs the same pure planner/ZIP code) with `notion-templates|notion-export|notion-import.spec.ts` and `pages-nav.spec.ts › "export with sub-pages and images"`. Review findings: `test/transfer-review.test.ts`, `test/transfer-sanitize.test.ts`.

## Shell parity, wave 4B (live updates, shortcuts, palette, page discussion)

- **Live updates into a PLAIN editor (NP-OF-05) — the rule.** Renderers read `note.content` once, at mount. Each autosave editor keeps its **OWN base revision** (`useAutoSave(…, {base: note.updatedAt, content: note.content, noWrite})`): the note it mounted from, moved only by its own CONFIRMED save (a queued/optimistic result, `_queued`, does not move it) or by the same content arriving under a newer revision (a metadata/path write — `rebase`). Every save names that base (`ifUpdatedAt`), never the query cache's `updatedAt` — an event re-read advances the cache while the editor still shows older text, which is how a dirty editor used to overwrite a remote edit with no 409. **Never pass a content save without the editor's base, and never read the base from the cache.** A 409 whose server copy is still exactly the content the editor is built on (only metadata/path/tags moved the revision — this tab's own icon write, a property set elsewhere) is not a conflict: the editor re-reads (`fresh`), moves its base and sends once more; any other 409 goes to "Needs review". `Canvas` judges every re-read with `remoteAdoption(noteId, {content, updatedAt})`: `own` (echo / same content) · `stale` (not STRICTLY newer than the base — an older copy is never shown) · `clean` → adopted SILENTLY only if nobody is in the page (no focus inside `<main>`, no selection, no IME composition, no title edit / find bar / menu / dialog), else offered · `dirty` → offered; the draft stays and its save 409s into "Needs review" · `draft` (typed in an editor that never writes: propose mode, read-only) → **never adopted**, notice only · `parked`/`failed` → offered. "Offered" = the document's existing review preview (`GhostTextOverlay`, now "This page was updated elsewhere — Show latest / Keep mine"; it is still how an external or agent edit is reviewed whenever it is not adopted silently, and outside the canvas it reviews every external change as before) or, for renderers without one, `RemoteUpdateBar`. "Show latest" adopts only if the editor is clean AT THAT MOMENT (else it flushes → the conflict path); adoption remounts, keeps the scroll position and writes nothing; an unmount flush uses the editor's old base. Live collab docs never take this path. `notion-live.spec.ts`, `notion-live-safety.spec.ts`.
- **Tree refresh on events (M1).** `/api/events` frames carry `tree: true` only when THIS viewer's sidebar row changed (created, removed, appeared/disappeared for them, path/tags/type/icon/order/trash — `treeRowChanged`, computed per connection after the same view filter, on the row before and after). The client refetches `["vault","tree"]` only for those (at once), for `resync`, and — older server — for an id the tree has never listed; never for a content edit, and not while the tab is hidden (one catch-up refetch when visible). Touched ids are marked for ONE `latest` re-read (`getNote(id, {latest:true})` → `cache: "reload"` → `Cache-Control: no-cache`).
- **Gateway read coalescing (H3).** `coalescedGet` has a write generation: `dropReadCache()` (every write path) bumps it and forgets in-flight reads, so a GET that started before an owner write can neither be joined by a later read nor store its pre-write body. A single-note GET with `Cache-Control: no-cache|no-store` skips the 5 s reuse window (lists never do). `test/gateway-read-cache.test.ts`.
- **Shortcuts (`useKeyboardShortcuts.ts`).** ⌘\ sidebar (by `e.code`, so Shift doesn't change it), ⌘⇧\ info panel, ⌘[ / ⌘] back/forward, ⌘⇧L theme (`toggleTheme()` reads the class on `<html>`), ⌘, Settings; **⌘B is Bold only** (the sidebar binding was removed at the 4A/4B integration). THE table is `lib/shortcuts.ts` `APP_SHORTCUTS` — the shortcut sheet rows and the ⌘K hints (`shortcutKeys(name)`) read it, and `notion-sidebar.spec` "the shortcut sheet's shell rows are the working bindings" presses each listed key. An editor that uses a key itself wins (`defaultPrevented`). Sidebar width + collapsed persist per device (`prism:sidebar`, written by the DESKTOP shell only — the phone drawer shares the flag and must never be remembered); tree expansion persists per account + vault (`prism:tree-expanded:<scope>`, cleared at sign-out with the other device-local keys in `readCache.ts`, which also fires `prism:signed-out` so the in-memory store resets).
- **Back/forward restores scroll (NP-SR-07).** `navBack`/`navForward` bump `navRestore`; `Canvas` remembers, per tab, the page-sized scroller inside `<main>` (renderers bring their own — capture-phase listener) and restores only on that bump, retrying until the page is tall enough (≤2 s), letting go on any wheel/touch/key/mouse. An ordinary open never restores (deep links scroll to their own anchor).
- **"Open in new tab"** = `openInNewTab()` (`lib/pages/openInNewTab.ts`): the page becomes a tab BEHIND the one being read (`openTabInBackground`) with a "Go to tab" toast — tree ⋯ menu, ⌘↵ in ⌘K. For the page already in front the page ⋯ menu opens its own address (`pageLink`) in a new browser tab (not offered in the native shell or the phone sheet).
- **Page icon everywhere (NP-PG-01).** `GET /api/tree` now emits `icon` (`metadata.icon`, ≤32 chars, else dropped) → `rest.listTree` → `metadata.icon` on tree rows; `<PageIcon noteId fallback>` (`lib/pages/icons.tsx`) reads the tree (renders the fallback without a query/vault client) and a device-local override store (`notePageIconChanged`, called by `useUpdateNote` and CollabDoc's icon write) so tabs, breadcrumbs, favorites, recents and the sidebar follow a change at once. The override is a bridge, not a second truth: a failed write clears it, and once the write is confirmed the next completed tree read replaces it (`reconcilePageIcons`).
- **Title + aliases in the tree (wikilink/mention matching).** `GET /api/tree` also emits `title` (`metadata.title`, non-blank, ≤ 200 chars, else dropped; NOT emitted when it equals the file name — the path leaf, with or without `.md`) and `aliases` (`metadata.aliases` + the single `metadata.alias`: up to 10 distinct non-blank strings of ≤ 100 chars; other entries dropped); C0 control and bidi control characters are stripped from both (`cleanText`, the `cleanLabel` rule). 🔒 The UNFILTERED admin tree still lists every path, but a PRIVATE row keeps its title/aliases unless the caller can VIEW it (`renderTree(tree, undefined, {viewer, canView})`; the cached body is per viewer) — never another member's private title. Same view filter, part of the ETag and of `treeRowChanged` (so `/api/events` `tree: true` fires on a title/alias change), in `TREE_META_KEYS` for the lean fallback. `rest.listTree` puts them in the row's `metadata`; a live document's `[[` / `@` suggestions read the tree through `apps/web/src/collab/linkNotes.ts` `useLinkNotes()` (never the full vault list) and match title, file name and aliases. Other tree readers that prefer `metadata.title` (canvas card pickers, template list, tree-backed wikilink resolution) now see it too. Tests: `test/tree.test.ts`, `test/events-tree-flag.test.ts`, `notion-mentions.spec.ts` "live document: …" (`?live`).
- **Initial JavaScript: `@prism/core/shell` vs `@prism/core` (NP-PF-08).** `packages/core/src/shell.ts` is everything a host needs to start the app and NOTHING that loads the block editor; `@prism/core` (`index.ts`) = the shell + `CollabEditor`, `CommentsSidebar`, `HumanSuggestionComposer`, `PresenceAvatars`, `PageDiscussion`, `MentionNode`. **Every module on the web app's boot path imports `@prism/core/shell`** (all of `apps/web/src` except the lazily loaded `collab/CollabDoc.tsx`); a static `@prism/core` import there puts TipTap, ProseMirror, Yjs and highlight.js back into the first chunk (417 KB gzip today, budget 600). Guard: `npm run check:initial -w @prism/web` (`perf-static-graph.mjs --assert-lazy`); measure: `npm run perf:bundle -w @prism/web` after a build. New shell exports go in `shell.ts`; an export that imports the editor goes in `index.ts`. `COLLAB_SCHEMA_VERSION` is defined in `editor/schemaVersion.ts` (no imports) and re-exported by `collabSchema.ts`. The live editor is mounted through `apps/web/src/collab/lazyCollab.tsx` (`preloadCollabEditor()` at the top of `start()` for a `/page/<id>` link, else on idle / first input; once loaded a document mounts it directly; it is a state loader, NOT `React.lazy` — a failed download is thrown to the `RendererBoundary` and its Retry remounts into a NEW download). `chunkReload.ts` (the `vite:preloadError` cache-bust + reload) does nothing while `navigator.onLine === false`. `collab/unsynced.ts` must stay Yjs-free (storage = `localDocumentStore.ts`, the headless push = `unsyncedSync.ts`, imported when a document is waiting). `syncUnsyncedDocs` re-checks the signed-in account FRESH before each document (`captureWriteContext(true)` + `sameScope`, like the outbox). The leave prompt's download reads raw storage (`exportLocalDocumentRaw`: `yjsUpdateBase64` = the IndexedDB row, `pendingUpdateBase64` = the rescue entry to apply on top) — no chunk needed; if a document's state cannot be read, `confirmLeaveWithUnsent` answers "stay" with a toast and nothing is purged. **Identity at boot:** `fetchMe()` always asks the server; `fetchMe({ maxAgeMs })` (and `captureWriteContext("recent")`) may reuse an identity confirmed ≤ 3 s ago or the request in flight — only for passes that re-check before sending (the outbox row selection, the unsynced-document pass, the account menu); the check before each queued write stays `captureWriteContext(true)`. **Web fonts:** `index.html` preloads the Google Fonts sheet and `bootstrap.ts` turns it into a stylesheet (never render-blocking; no inline `onload`, the CSP has no inline script).
- **Favorites order (NP-SB-04)** is the synced list's order: `preferenceOps.moveFavorite` / `moveWithin(list, id, index, visible)` — ids hidden from the caller keep following the visible id before them, never lost. Drag, or Alt+Shift+↑/↓ on a focused row (announced). Recents: stored ≤50 (headroom for hidden ids), shown ≤12.
- **⌘K.** Ranked + keyword run together and are blended (`lib/search/blend.ts`: title matches, then ranked, then other keyword hits; a page found by both keeps its ranked row; either side may fail alone — mode `blended|ranked|fallback|keyword`). The rows on screen stay while the next answer is on its way (typing ahead of the 200 ms debounce, a background refetch): `useVaultSearch` hands back the answer THIS list was just showing (`held: true`; same audience + same filters, ≤ `SEARCH_HOLD_MS` 10 s, cleared by an error or an empty query) — never an answer straight from the query cache — and Enter acts on the selected visible row. **A mouse press on a row is on the ITEM, not on a place:** it is remembered by id at `pointerdown` and runs at `pointerup` when the button comes up on its row, or anywhere in the dialog once the row has moved since the press (results landing above it, the filter bar appearing — the browser makes no click then); a drag off an unmoved row cancels and the row the pointer ends on is never opened; touch, assistive tech and scripts keep the plain click (`pressRow` / `clickRow` in `CommandBar.tsx`; `notion-search.spec.ts` "⌘K: …"). "Moved" is judged in the LIST's coordinates (`placeOf`: the row's offset in the scroller's content + the scroller's own top) — scrolling the list (a wheel, `scrollIntoView`) is not a move. A pending press is forgotten by Enter, by a `pointerdown` that is not on a row, by `contextmenu`, and when the window blurs or hides. Rows show the edited date; commands carry `keys` → a hidden `<kbd>` + `aria-keyshortcuts` (the row's accessible name stays the label); the selected page can be starred without opening it. Phone search is full-screen, sized to `visualViewport`.
- **Page-level discussion (NP-CO-02).** A page thread is an ordinary `comments` Y.Map entry with `page: true`, `quote: ""` and NO `comment` mark — no editor schema change, no version bump. Editors write it over their socket (`createPageThread`); suggest-level people and link guests send the strict `{kind:"page-comment", text}` command (`POST /api/collab/:id/commands`: suggest required, comment/view → 403, extra keys → 400, same thread/size budgets, receipts, replay-once; lost-command cleanup removes the item and keeps the thread only if someone else replied). Reply/resolve/delete are the existing commands. UI `PageDiscussion.tsx` (under the title in CollabDoc; the sidebar lists it as "Page comment"). Tests: `test/human-collab.test.ts` (page-comment…), `notion-comments.spec.ts`.
- **Smaller pieces.** Title Enter commits and focuses the body (`DocumentChrome` `EditableTitle`); the outline marks the section being read (`aria-current="location"`, measured from the DOM while open); "Offline copy from <time>" (`OfflineCopyNotice`: the host stamps `_offlineCopyAt` on a note served from the read cache — `x-prism-cache-stored` — or the device is offline and the copy is this session's); the phone page sheet adds Share / Find in page / Agent (`usePageMenuItems({sheet:true})`; Find is the editor group's ONE `find-in-page` menu item → `requestFindInPage()` in `lib/tiptap/findShortcuts.ts`, the only implementation of the `prism:find-in-page` event; 4B's `lib/editor/findInPage.ts`, its extra sheet row and its second editor listeners were removed at integration); a disabled menu action shows its reason in the menu; Share → Publish explains per-tag publishing, lists the pages YOU can see with the tag (labelled as your view, not the published set) and hands off to the Publishing studio (`PublishHandoff`; the dialog's direct publish controls remain); a new page seeds its own create response into the note cache (focused title < 300 ms, no read-back).
